% ----------------------
\subsection{Job}
% ----------------------
	\label{JOB}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Job 1} % *JOB-1
	% -----
		\label{JOB-1}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:1} % *JOB-1-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-1}

			There was a man in the land of Uz, whose name was Job; and that man was perfect and upright, and one that feared God, and eschewed evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֛ישׁ הָיָ֥ה בְאֶֽרֶץ־ע֖וּץ אִיּ֣וֹב שְׁמ֑וֹ וְהָיָ֣ה ׀ הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֗וּא תָּ֧ם וְיָשָׁ֛ר וִירֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְסָ֥ר מֵרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There was a man in the land of Uz, whose name was Job; and that man was perfect and upright, and one that feared God, and turned away from evil.
					\item[NIV] In the land of Uz there lived a man whose name was Job. This man was blameless and upright; he feared God and shunned evil.
					\item[NRSV] There was once a man in the land of Uz whose name was Job. That man was blameless and upright, one who feared God and turned away from evil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:2} % *JOB-1-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-2}

			And there were born unto him seven sons and three daughters.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּוָּ֥לְדוּ ל֛וֹ שִׁבְעָ֥ה בָנִ֖ים וְשָׁל֥וֹשׁ בָּנֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there were born unto him seven sons and three daughters.
					\item[NIV] He had seven sons and three daughters,
					\item[NRSV] There were born to him seven sons and three daughters.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:3} % *JOB-1-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-3}

			His substance also was seven thousand sheep, and three thousand camels, and five hundred yoke of oxen, and five hundred she asses, and a very great household; so that this man was the greatest of all the men of the east.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י מִ֠קְנֵהוּ שִֽׁבְעַ֨ת אַלְפֵי־צֹ֜אן וּשְׁלֹ֧שֶׁת אַלְפֵ֣י גְמַלִּ֗ים וַחֲמֵ֨שׁ מֵא֤וֹת צֶֽמֶד־בָּקָר֙ וַחֲמֵ֣שׁ מֵא֣וֹת אֲתוֹנ֔וֹת וַעֲבֻדָּ֖ה רַבָּ֣ה מְאֹ֑ד וַיְהִי֙ הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא גָּד֖וֹל מִכָּל־בְּנֵי־קֶֽדֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] His substance also was seven thousand sheep, and three thousand camels, and five hundred yoke of oxen, and five hundred she-asses, and a very great household; so that this man was the greatest of all the children of the east.
					\item[NIV] and he owned seven thousand sheep, three thousand camels, five hundred yoke of oxen and five hundred donkeys, and had a large number of servants. He was the greatest man among all the people of the East.
					\item[NRSV] He had seven thousand sheep, three thousand camels, five hundred yoke of oxen, five hundred donkeys, and very many servants; so that this man was the greatest of all the people of the east.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:4} % *JOB-1-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-4}

			And his sons went and feasted in their houses, every one his day; and sent and called for their three sisters to eat and to drink with them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְהָלְכ֤וּ בָנָיו֙ וְעָשׂ֣וּ מִשְׁתֶּ֔ה בֵּ֖ית אִ֣ישׁ יוֹמ֑וֹ וְשָׁלְח֗וּ וְקָרְאוּ֙ לִשְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת אַחְיֽוֹתֵיהֶ֔ם לֶאֱכֹ֥ל וְלִשְׁתּ֖וֹת עִמָּהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And his sons went and held a feast in the house of each one upon his day; and they Sent and called for their three sisters to eat and to drink with them.
					\item[NIV] His sons used to hold feasts in their homes on their birthdays, and they would invite their three sisters to eat and drink with them.
					\item[NRSV] His sons used to go and hold feasts in one another’s houses in turn; and they would send and invite their three sisters to eat and drink with them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:5} % *JOB-1-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-5}

			And it was so, when the days of their feasting were gone about, that Job sent and sanctified them, and rose up early in the morning, and offered burnt offerings according to the number of them all: for Job said, It may be that my sons have sinned, and cursed God in their hearts. Thus did Job continually.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֡י כִּ֣י הִקִּיפֽוּ֩ יְמֵ֨י הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֜ה וַיִּשְׁלַ֧ח אִיּ֣וֹב וַֽיְקַדְּשֵׁ֗ם וְהִשְׁכִּ֣ים בַּבֹּקֶר֮ וְהֶעֱלָ֣ה עֹלוֹת֮ מִסְפַּ֣ר כֻּלָּם֒ כִּ֚י אָמַ֣ר אִיּ֔וֹב אוּלַי֙ חָטְא֣וּ בָנַ֔י וּבֵרֲכ֥וּ אֱלֹהִ֖ים בִּלְבָבָ֑ם כָּ֛כָה יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה אִיּ֖וֹב כָּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it was so, when the days of their feasting were gone about, that Job sent and sanctified them, and rose up early in the morning, and offered burnt-offerings according to the number of them all: for Job said, It may be that my sons have sinned, and renounced God in their hearts. Thus did Job continually.
					\item[NIV] When a period of feasting had run its course, Job would make arrangements for them to be purified. Early in the morning he would sacrifice a burnt offering for each of them, thinking, “Perhaps my children have sinned and cursed God in their hearts.” This was Job’s regular custom.
					\item[NRSV] And when the feast days had run their course, Job would send and sanctify them, and he would rise early in the morning and offer burnt offerings according to the number of them all; for Job said, “It may be that my children have sinned, and cursed God in their hearts.” This is what Job always did.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:6} % *JOB-1-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-6}

			Now there was a day when the sons of God came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan came also among them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י הַיּ֔וֹם וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ בְּנֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב עַל־יְהוָ֑ה וַיָּב֥וֹא גַֽם־הַשָּׂטָ֖ן בְּתוֹכָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now it came to pass on the day when the sons of God came to present themselves before Jehovah, that Satan also came among them.
					\item[NIV] One day the angels came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan also came with them.
					\item[NRSV] One day the heavenly beings came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan also came among them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:7} % *JOB-1-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-7}

			And the LORD said unto Satan, Whence comest thou? Then Satan answered the LORD, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֧אמֶר יְהוָ֛ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֖ן מֵאַ֣יִן תָּבֹ֑א וַיַּ֨עַן הַשָּׂטָ֤ן אֶת־יְהוָה֙ וַיֹּאמַ֔ר מִשּׁ֣וּט בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּמֵֽהִתְהַלֵּ֖ךְ בָּֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Jehovah said unto Satan, Whence comest thou? Then Satan answered Jehovah, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it.
					\item[NIV] The LORD said to Satan, “Where have you come from?” Satan answered the LORD, “From roaming throughout the earth, going back and forth on it.”
					\item[NRSV] The LORD said to Satan, “Where have you come from?” Satan answered the LORD, “From going to and fro on the earth, and from walking up and down on it.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:8} % *JOB-1-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-8}

			And the LORD said unto Satan, Hast thou considered my servant Job, that there is none like him in the earth, a perfect and an upright man, one that feareth God, and escheweth evil?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהוָה֙ אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֔ן הֲשַׂ֥מְתָּ לִבְּךָ֖ עַל־עַבְדִּ֣י אִיּ֑וֹב כִּ֣י אֵ֤ין כָּמֹ֙הוּ֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ אִ֣ישׁ תָּ֧ם וְיָשָׁ֛ר יְרֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְסָ֥ר מֵרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Jehovah said unto Satan, Hast thou considered my servant Job? for there is none like him in the earth, a perfect and an upright man, one that feareth God, and turneth away from evil.
					\item[NIV] Then the LORD said to Satan, “Have you considered my servant Job? There is no one on earth like him; he is blameless and upright, a man who fears God and shuns evil.”
					\item[NRSV] The LORD said to Satan, “Have you considered my servant Job? There is no one like him on the earth, a blameless and upright man who fears God and turns away from evil.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:9} % *JOB-1-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-9}

			Then Satan answered the LORD, and said, Doth Job fear God for nought?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֧עַן הַשָּׂטָ֛ן אֶת־יְהוָ֖ה וַיֹּאמַ֑ר הַֽחִנָּ֔ם יָרֵ֥א אִיּ֖וֹב אֱלֹהִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then Satan answered Jehovah, and said, Doth Job fear God for nought?
					\item[NIV] “Does Job fear God for nothing?” Satan replied.
					\item[NRSV] Then Satan answered the LORD, “Does Job fear God for nothing?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:10} % *JOB-1-10
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-10}

			Hast not thou made an hedge about him, and about his house, and about all that he hath on every side? thou hast blessed the work of his hands, and his substance is increased in the land.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַ֠תָּה שַׂ֣כְתָּ בַעֲד֧וֹ וּבְעַד־בֵּית֛וֹ וּבְעַ֥ד כָּל־אֲשֶׁר־ל֖וֹ מִסָּבִ֑יב מַעֲשֵׂ֤ה יָדָיו֙ בֵּרַ֔כְתָּ וּמִקְנֵ֖הוּ פָּרַ֥ץ בָּאָֽרֶץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hast not thou made a hedge about him, and about his house, and about all that he hath, on every side? thou hast blessed the work of his hands, and his substance is increased in the land.
					\item[NIV] “Have you not put a hedge around him and his household and everything he has? You have blessed the work of his hands, so that his flocks and herds are spread throughout the land.
					\item[NRSV] Have you not put a fence around him and his house and all that he has, on every side? You have blessed the work of his hands, and his possessions have increased in the land.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:11} % *JOB-1-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-11}

			But put forth thine hand now, and touch all that he hath, and he will curse thee to thy face.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאוּלָם֙ שְֽׁלַֽח־נָ֣א יָֽדְךָ֔ וְגַ֖ע בְּכָל־אֲשֶׁר־ל֑וֹ אִם־לֹ֥א עַל־פָּנֶ֖יךָ יְבָרֲכֶֽךָּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But put forth thy hand now, and touch all that he hath, and he will renounce thee to thy face.
					\item[NIV] But now stretch out your hand and strike everything he has, and he will surely curse you to your face.”
					\item[NRSV] But stretch out your hand now, and touch all that he has, and he will curse you to your face.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:12} % *JOB-1-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-12}

			And the LORD said unto Satan, Behold, all that he hath is in thy power; only upon himself put not forth thine hand. So Satan went forth from the presence of the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהוָ֜ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֗ן הִנֵּ֤ה כָל־אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ֙ בְּיָדֶ֔ךָ רַ֣ק אֵלָ֔יו אַל־תִּשְׁלַ֖ח יָדֶ֑ךָ וַיֵּצֵא֙ הַשָּׂטָ֔ן מֵעִ֖ם פְּנֵ֥י יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Jehovah said unto Satan, Behold, all that he hath is in thy power; only upon himself put not forth thy hand. So Satan went forth from the presence of Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] The LORD said to Satan, “Very well, then, everything he has is in your power, but on the man himself do not lay a finger.” Then Satan went out from the presence of the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD said to Satan, “Very well, all that he has is in your power; only do not stretch out your hand against him!” So Satan went out from the presence of the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:13} % *JOB-1-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-13}

			And there was a day when his sons and his daughters were eating and drinking wine in their eldest brother’s house:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֖י הַיּ֑וֹם וּבָנָ֨יו וּבְנֹתָ֤יו אֹֽכְלִים֙ וְשֹׁתִ֣ים יַ֔יִן בְּבֵ֖ית אֲחִיהֶ֥ם הַבְּכֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it fell on a day when his sons and his daughters were eating and drinking wine in their eldest brother's house,
					\item[NIV] One day when Job’s sons and daughters were feasting and drinking wine at the oldest brother’s house,
					\item[NRSV] One day when his sons and daughters were eating and drinking wine in the eldest brother’s house,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 1:14} % *JOB-1-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-14}

			And there came a messenger unto Job, and said, The oxen were plowing, and the asses feeding beside them:

			\begin{versebox}

			And the Sabeans fell upon them, and took them away; yea, they have slain the servants with the edge of the sword; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.

			While he was yet speaking, there came also another, and said, The fire of God is fallen from heaven, and hath burned up the sheep, and the servants, and consumed them; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ע֣וֹד ׀ זֶ֣ה מְדַבֵּ֗ר וְזֶה֮ בָּ֣א וַיֹּאמַר֒ אֵ֣שׁ אֱלֹהִ֗ים נָֽפְלָה֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַתִּבְעַ֥ר בַּצֹּ֛אן וּבַנְּעָרִ֖ים וַתֹּאכְלֵ֑ם וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃}

					\item[NRSV] While he was still speaking, another came and said, “The fire of God fell from heaven and burned up the sheep and the servants, and consumed them; I alone have escaped to tell you.”

			While he was yet speaking, there came also another, and said, The Chaldeans made out three bands, and fell upon the camels, and have carried them away, yea, and slain the servants with the edge of the sword; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ע֣וֹד ׀ זֶ֣ה מְדַבֵּ֗ר וְזֶה֮ בָּ֣א וַיֹּאמַר֒ כַּשְׂדִּ֞ים שָׂ֣מוּ ׀ שְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה רָאשִׁ֗ים וַֽיִּפְשְׁט֤וּ עַל־הַגְּמַלִּים֙ וַיִּקָּח֔וּם וְאֶת־הַנְּעָרִ֖ים הִכּ֣וּ לְפִי־חָ֑רֶב וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃}

					\item[NIV] While he was still speaking, another messenger came and said, “The Chaldeans formed three raiding parties and swept down on your camels and made off with them. They put the servants to the sword, and I am the only one who has escaped to tell you!”
					\item[NRSV] While he was still speaking, another came and said, “The Chaldeans formed three columns, made a raid on the camels and carried them off, and killed the servants with the edge of the sword; I alone have escaped to tell you.”

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-1-19}

			And, behold, there came a great wind from the wilderness, and smote the four corners of the house, and it fell upon the young men, and they are dead; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְהִנֵּה֩ ר֨וּחַ גְּדוֹלָ֜ה בָּ֣אָה ׀ מֵעֵ֣בֶר הַמִּדְבָּ֗ר וַיִּגַּע֙ בְּאַרְבַּע֙ פִּנּ֣וֹת הַבַּ֔יִת וַיִּפֹּ֥ל עַל־הַנְּעָרִ֖ים וַיָּמ֑וּתוּ וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃}

					\item[NIV] when suddenly a mighty wind swept in from the desert and struck the four corners of the house. It collapsed on them and they are dead, and I am the only one who has escaped to tell you!”
					\item[NRSV] and suddenly a great wind came across the desert, struck the four corners of the house, and it fell on the young people, and they are dead; I alone have escaped to tell you.”

			And said, Naked came I out of my mother’s womb, and naked shall I return thither: the LORD gave, and the LORD hath taken away; blessed be the name of the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּאמֶר֩ עָרֹ֨ם יָצָ֜אתִי מִבֶּ֣טֶן אִמִּ֗י וְעָרֹם֙ אָשׁ֣וּב שָׁ֔מָה יְהוָ֣ה נָתַ֔ן וַיהוָ֖ה לָקָ֑ח יְהִ֛י שֵׁ֥ם יְהוָ֖ה מְבֹרָֽךְ׃}

					\item[NRSV] He said, “Naked I came from my mother’s womb, and naked shall I return there; the LORD gave, and the LORD has taken away; blessed be the name of the LORD.”

			In all this Job sinned not, nor charged God foolishly.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Job 2} % *JOB-2
	% -----
		\label{JOB-2}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:1} % *JOB-2-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-1}

			Again there was a day when the sons of God came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan came also among them to present himself before the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י הַיּ֔וֹם וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ בְּנֵ֣י הָֽאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב עַל־יְהוָ֑ה וַיָּב֤וֹא גַֽם־הַשָּׂטָן֙ בְּתֹכָ֔ם לְהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב עַל־יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Again it came to pass on the day when the sons of God came to present themselves before Jehovah, that Satan came also among them to present himself before Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] On another day the angels came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan also came with them to present himself before him.
					\item[NRSV] One day the heavenly beings came to present themselves before the LORD, and Satan also came among them to present himself before the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:2} % *JOB-2-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-2}

			And the LORD said unto Satan, From whence comest thou? And Satan answered the LORD, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהוָה֙ אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֔ן אֵ֥י מִזֶּ֖ה תָּבֹ֑א וַיַּ֨עַן הַשָּׂטָ֤ן אֶת־יְהוָה֙ וַיֹּאמַ֔ר מִשֻּׁ֣ט בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּמֵהִתְהַלֵּ֖ךְ בָּֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Jehovah said unto Satan, From whence comest thou? And Satan answered Jehovah, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it.
					\item[NIV] And the LORD said to Satan, “Where have you come from?” Satan answered the LORD, “From roaming throughout the earth, going back and forth on it.”
					\item[NRSV] The LORD said to Satan, “Where have you come from?” Satan answered the LORD, “From going to and fro on the earth, and from walking up and down on it.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:3} % *JOB-2-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-3}

			And the LORD said unto Satan, Hast thou considered my servant Job, that there is none like him in the earth, a perfect and an upright man, one that feareth God, and escheweth evil? and still he holdeth fast his integrity, although thou movedst me against him, to destroy him without cause.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהוָ֜ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֗ן הֲשַׂ֣מְתָּ לִבְּךָ֮ אֶל־עַבְדִּ֣י אִיּוֹב֒ כִּי֩ אֵ֨ין כָּמֹ֜הוּ בָּאָ֗רֶץ אִ֣ישׁ תָּ֧ם וְיָשָׁ֛ר יְרֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְסָ֣ר מֵרָ֑ע וְעֹדֶ֙נּוּ֙ מַחֲזִ֣יק בְּתֻמָּת֔וֹ וַתְּסִיתֵ֥נִי ב֖וֹ לְבַלְּע֥וֹ חִנָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Jehovah said unto Satan, Hast thou considered my servant Job? for there is none like him in the earth, a perfect and an upright man, one that feareth God, and turneth away from evil: and he still holdeth fast his integrity, although thou movedst me against him, to destroy him without cause.
					\item[NIV] Then the LORD said to Satan, “Have you considered my servant Job? There is no one on earth like him; he is blameless and upright, a man who fears God and shuns evil. And he still maintains his integrity, though you incited me against him to ruin him without any reason.”
					\item[NRSV] The LORD said to Satan, “Have you considered my servant Job? There is no one like him on the earth, a blameless and upright man who fears God and turns away from evil. He still persists in his integrity, although you incited me against him, to destroy him for no reason.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:4} % *JOB-2-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-4}

			And Satan answered the LORD, and said, Skin for skin, yea, all that a man hath will he give for his life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֧עַן הַשָּׂטָ֛ן אֶת־יְהוָ֖ה וַיֹּאמַ֑ר ע֣וֹר בְּעַד־ע֗וֹר וְכֹל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָאִ֔ישׁ יִתֵּ֖ן בְּעַ֥ד נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Satan answered Jehovah, and said, Skin for skin, yea, all that a man hath will he give for his life.
					\item[NIV] “Skin for skin!” Satan replied. “A man will give all he has for his own life.
					\item[NRSV] Then Satan answered the LORD, “Skin for skin! All that people have they will give to save their lives.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:5} % *JOB-2-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-5}

			But put forth thine hand now, and touch his bone and his flesh, and he will curse thee to thy face.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אוּלָם֙ שְֽׁלַֽח־נָ֣א יָֽדְךָ֔ וְגַ֥ע אֶל־עַצְמ֖וֹ וְאֶל־בְּשָׂר֑וֹ אִם־לֹ֥א אֶל־פָּנֶ֖יךָ יְבָרֲכֶֽךָּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] But now stretch out your hand and strike his flesh and bones, and he will surely curse you to your face.”
					\item[NRSV] But stretch out your hand now and touch his bone and his flesh, and he will curse you to your face.”

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-6}

			And the LORD said unto Satan, Behold, he is in thine hand; but save his life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֧אמֶר יְהוָ֛ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֖ן הִנּ֣וֹ בְיָדֶ֑ךָ אַ֖ךְ אֶת־נַפְשׁ֥וֹ שְׁמֹֽר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] The LORD said to Satan, “Very well, then, he is in your hands; but you must spare his life.”
					\item[NRSV] The LORD said to Satan, “Very well, he is in your power; only spare his life.”

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-7}

			So went Satan forth from the presence of the LORD, and smote Job with sore boils from the sole of his foot unto his crown.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּצֵא֙ הַשָּׂטָ֔ן מֵאֵ֖ת פְּנֵ֣י יְהוָ֑ה וַיַּ֤ךְ אֶת־אִיּוֹב֙ בִּשְׁחִ֣ין רָ֔ע מִכַּ֥ף רַגְל֖וֹ וְעַ֥ד קָדְקֳדֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NRSV] So Satan went out from the presence of the LORD, and inflicted loathsome sores on Job from the sole of his foot to the crown of his head.
					% % \item[OTX*]

			And he took him a potsherd to scrape himself withal; and he sat down among the ashes.

			\begin{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 2:9} % *JOB-2-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-9}

			Then said his wife unto him, Dost thou still retain thine integrity? curse God, and die.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC45-32]{DC 45:32} and \hyperref[MORMON-2-14]{Mormon 2:14}.
					}

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ אִשְׁתּ֔וֹ עֹדְךָ֖ מַחֲזִ֣יק בְּתֻמָּתֶ֑ךָ בָּרֵ֥ךְ אֱלֹהִ֖ים וָמֻֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said his wife unto him, Dost thou still hold fast thine integrity? renounce God, and die.
					\item[NIV] His wife said to him, “Are you still maintaining your integrity? Curse God and die!”
					\item[NRSV] Then his wife said to him, “Do you still persist in your integrity? Curse God, and die.”

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:10} % *JOB-2-10
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-10}

			But he said unto her, Thou speakest as one of the foolish women speaketh. What? shall we receive good at the hand of God, and shall we not receive evil? In all this did not Job sin with his lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלֶ֗יהָ כְּדַבֵּ֞ר אַחַ֤ת הַנְּבָלוֹת֙ תְּדַבֵּ֔רִי גַּ֣ם אֶת־הַטּ֗וֹב נְקַבֵּל֙ מֵאֵ֣ת הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וְאֶת־הָרָ֖ע לֹ֣א נְקַבֵּ֑ל בְּכָל־זֹ֛את לֹא־חָטָ֥א אִיּ֖וֹב בִּשְׂפָתָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But he said unto her, Thou speakest as one of the foolish women speaketh. What? shall we receive good at the hand of God, and shall we not receive evil? In all this did not Job sin with his lips.
					\item[NIV] He replied, “You are talking like a foolish woman. Shall we accept good from God, and not trouble?” In all this, Job did not sin in what he said.
					\item[NRSV] But he said to her, “You speak as any foolish woman would speak. Shall we receive the good at the hand of God, and not receive the bad?” In all this Job did not sin with his lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:11} % *JOB-2-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-11}

			Now when Job’s three friends heard of all this evil that was come upon him, they came every one from his own place; Eliphaz the Temanite, and Bildad the Shuhite, and Zophar the Naamathite: for they had made an appointment together to come to mourn with him and to comfort him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיִּשְׁמְע֞וּ שְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת ׀ רֵעֵ֣י אִיּ֗וֹב אֵ֣ת כָּל־הָרָעָ֣ה הַזֹּאת֮ הַבָּ֣אָה עָלָיו֒ וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ מִמְּקֹמ֔וֹ אֱלִיפַ֤ז הַתֵּימָנִי֙ וּבִלְדַּ֣ד הַשּׁוּחִ֔י וְצוֹפַ֖ר הַנַּֽעֲמָתִ֑י וַיִּוָּעֲד֣וּ יַחְדָּ֔ו לָב֥וֹא לָנֽוּד־ל֖וֹ וּֽלְנַחֲמֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now when Job's three friends heard of all this evil that was come upon him, they came every one from his own place: Eliphaz the Temanite, and Bildad the Shuhite, and Zophar the Naamathite, and they made an appointment together to come to bemoan him and to comfort him.
					\item[NIV] When Job’s three friends, Eliphaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite, heard about all the troubles that had come upon him, they set out from their homes and met together by agreement to go and sympathize with him and comfort him.
					\item[NRSV] Now when Job’s three friends heard of all these troubles that had come upon him, each of them set out from his home—Eliphaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuhite, and Zophar the Naamathite. They met together to go and console and comfort him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:12} % *JOB-2-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-12}

			And when they lifted up their eyes afar off, and knew him not, they lifted up their voice, and wept; and they rent every one his mantle, and sprinkled dust upon their heads toward heaven.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּשְׂא֨וּ אֶת־עֵינֵיהֶ֤ם מֵרָחוֹק֙ וְלֹ֣א הִכִּירֻ֔הוּ וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ קוֹלָ֖ם וַיִּבְכּ֑וּ וַֽיִּקְרְעוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ מְעִל֔וֹ וַיִּזְרְק֥וּ עָפָ֛ר עַל־רָאשֵׁיהֶ֖ם הַשָּׁמָֽיְמָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And when they lifted up their eyes afar off, and knew him not, they lifted up their voice, and wept; and they rent every one his robe, and sprinkled dust upon their heads toward heaven.
					\item[NIV] When they saw him from a distance, they could hardly recognize him; they began to weep aloud, and they tore their robes and sprinkled dust on their heads.
					\item[NRSV] When they saw him from a distance, they did not recognize him, and they raised their voices and wept aloud; they tore their robes and threw dust in the air upon their heads.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 2:13} % *JOB-2-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-2-13}

			So they sat down with him upon the ground seven days and seven nights, and none spake a word unto him: for they saw that his grief was very great.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּשְׁב֤וּ אִתּוֹ֙ לָאָ֔רֶץ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים וְשִׁבְעַ֣ת לֵיל֑וֹת וְאֵין־דֹּבֵ֤ר אֵלָיו֙ דָּבָ֔ר כִּ֣י רָא֔וּ כִּֽי־גָדַ֥ל הַכְּאֵ֖ב מְאֹֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So they sat down with him upon the ground seven days and seven nights, and none spake a word unto him: for they saw that his grief was very great.
					\item[NIV] Then they sat on the ground with him for seven days and seven nights. No one said a word to him, because they saw how great his suffering was.
					\item[NRSV] They sat with him on the ground seven days and seven nights, and no one spoke a word to him, for they saw that his suffering was very great.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

	\subsubsection{Job 3} % *JOB-3
	% -----
		\label{JOB-3}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 3:1} % *JOB-3-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-3-1}

			After this opened Job his mouth, and cursed his day.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַחֲרֵי־כֵ֗ן פָּתַ֤ח אִיּוֹב֙ אֶת־פִּ֔יהוּ וַיְקַלֵּ֖ל אֶת־יוֹמֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] After this opened Job his mouth, and cursed his day.
					\item[NIV] After this, Job opened his mouth and cursed the day of his birth.
					\item[NRSV] After this Job opened his mouth and cursed the day of his birth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 3:2} % *JOB-3-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-3-2}

			And Job spake, and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Job answered and said:
					\item[NIV] He said:
					\item[NRSV] Job said:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 3:3} % *JOB-3-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-3-3}

			Let the day perish wherein I was born, and the night in which it was said, There is a man child conceived.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֹ֣אבַד י֭וֹם אִוָּ֣לֶד בּ֑וֹ וְהַלַּ֥יְלָה אָ֝מַ֗ר הֹ֣רָה גָֽבֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let the day perish wherein I was born, And the night which said, There is a man-child conceived.
					\item[NIV] “May the day of my birth perish, and the night that said, ‘A boy is conceived!’
					\item[NRSV] “Let the day perish in which I was born, and the night that said, ‘A man-child is conceived.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 3:4} % *JOB-3-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-3-4}

			Let that day be darkness; let not God regard it from above, neither let the light shine upon it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַיּ֥וֹם הַה֗וּא יְֽהִ֫י חֹ֥שֶׁךְ אַֽל־יִדְרְשֵׁ֣הוּ אֱל֣וֹהַּ מִמָּ֑עַל וְאַל־תּוֹפַ֖ע עָלָ֣יו נְהָרָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let that day be darkness; Let not God from above seek for it, Neither let the light shine upon it.
					\item[NIV] That day—may it turn to darkness; may God above not care about it; may no light shine on it.
					\item[NRSV] Let that day be darkness! May God above not seek it, or light shine on it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 3:5} % *JOB-3-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-3-5}

			Let darkness and the shadow of death stain it; let a cloud dwell upon it; let the blackness of the day terrify it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִגְאָלֻ֡הוּ חֹ֣שֶׁךְ וְ֭צַלְמָוֶת תִּשְׁכָּן־עָלָ֣יו עֲנָנָ֑ה יְ֝בַעֲתֻ֗הוּ כִּֽמְרִ֥ירֵי יֽוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let darkness and the shadow of death claim it for their own; Let a cloud dwell upon it; Let all that maketh black the day terrify it.
					\item[NIV] May gloom and utter darkness claim it once more; may a cloud settle over it; may blackness overwhelm it.
					\item[NRSV] Let gloom and deep darkness claim it. Let clouds settle upon it; let the blackness of the day terrify it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 3:6} % *JOB-3-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-3-6}

			As for that night, let darkness seize upon it; let it not be joined unto the days of the year, let it not come into the number of the months.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַלַּ֥יְלָה הַהוּא֮ יִקָּחֵ֪ה֫וּ אֹ֥פֶל אַל־יִ֭חַדְּ בִּימֵ֣י שָׁנָ֑ה בְּמִסְפַּ֥ר יְ֝רָחִ֗ים אַל־יָבֹֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

			For the thing which I greatly feared is come upon me, and that which I was afraid of is come unto me.

			\begin{versebox}

	\subsubsection{Job 6} % *JOB-6
	% -----
		\label{JOB-6}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 6:1} % *JOB-6-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-6-1}

			But Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then Job answered and said,
					\item[NIV] Then Job replied:
					\item[NRSV] Then Job answered:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 6:2} % *JOB-6-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-6-2}

			Oh that my grief were throughly weighed, and my calamity laid in the balances together!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ל֗וּ שָׁק֣וֹל יִשָּׁקֵ֣ל כַּעְשִׂ֑י וְ֝הַוָּתִ֗י בְּֽמֹאזְנַ֥יִם יִשְׂאוּ־יָֽחַד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Oh that my vexation were but weighed, And all my calamity laid in the balances!
					\item[NIV] “If only my anguish could be weighed and all my misery be placed on the scales!
					\item[NRSV] “O that my vexation were weighed, and all my calamity laid in the balances!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 6:3} % *JOB-6-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-6-3}

			For now it would be heavier than the sand of the sea: therefore my words are swallowed up.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־עַתָּ֗ה מֵח֣וֹל יַמִּ֣ים יִכְבָּ֑ד עַל־כֵּ֝֗ן דְּבָרַ֥י לָֽעוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For now it would be heavier than the sand of the seas: Therefore have my words been rash.
					\item[NIV] It would surely outweigh the sand of the seas— no wonder my words have been impetuous.
					\item[NRSV] For then it would be heavier than the sand of the sea; therefore my words have been rash.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 6:4} % *JOB-6-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-6-4}

			For the arrows of the Almighty are within me, the poison whereof drinketh up my spirit: the terrors of God do set themselves in array against me.

			\begin{versebox}

			Did I say, Bring unto me? or, Give a reward for me of your substance?

					\item[NRSV] Have I said, ‘Make me a gift’? Or, ‘From your wealth offer a bribe for me’?

			Teach me, and I will hold my tongue: and cause me to understand wherein I have erred.

			\label{JOB-6-25}

			How forcible are right words! but what doth your arguing reprove?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַה־נִּמְרְצ֥וּ אִמְרֵי־יֹ֑שֶׁר וּמַה־יּוֹכִ֖יחַ הוֹכֵ֣חַ מִכֶּֽם׃}

					\item[NIV] How painful are honest words! But what do your arguments prove?
					\item[NRSV] How forceful are honest words! But your reproof, what does it reprove?

			Do ye imagine to reprove words, and the speeches of one that is desperate, which are as wind?

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] Do you mean to correct what I say, and treat my desperate words as wind?
					\item[NRSV] Do you think that you can reprove words, as if the speech of the desperate were wind?

			Return, I pray you, let it not be iniquity; yea, return again, my righteousness is in it.

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] Relent, do not be unjust; reconsider, for my integrity is at stake.
					\item[NRSV] Turn, I pray, let no wrong be done. Turn now, my vindication is at stake.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 7:1} % *JOB-7-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-7-1}

			Is there not an appointed time to man upon earth? are not his days also like the days of an hireling?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲלֹא־צָבָ֣א לֶאֱנ֣וֹשׁ על־[עֲלֵי־] אָ֑רֶץ וְכִימֵ֖י שָׂכִ֣יר יָמָֽיו׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] “Do not mortals have hard service on earth? Are not their days like those of hired laborers?
					\item[NRSV] “Do not human beings have a hard service on earth, and are not their days like the days of a laborer?

			As a servant earnestly desireth the shadow, and as an hireling looketh for the reward of his work:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּעֶ֥בֶד יִשְׁאַף־צֵ֑ל וּ֝כְשָׂכִ֗יר יְקַוֶּ֥ה פָעֳלֽוֹ׃}

					\item[NIV] Like a slave longing for the evening shadows, or a hired laborer waiting to be paid,
					\item[NRSV] Like a slave who longs for the shadow, and like laborers who look for their wages,

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-7-3}

			So am I made to possess months of vanity, and wearisome nights are appointed to me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֵּ֤ן הָנְחַ֣לְתִּי לִ֭י יַרְחֵי־שָׁ֑וְא וְלֵיל֥וֹת עָ֝מָ֗ל מִנּוּ־לִֽי׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] so I have been allotted months of futility, and nights of misery have been assigned to me.
					\item[NRSV] so I am allotted months of emptiness, and nights of misery are apportioned to me.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-7-4}

			When I lie down, I say, When shall I arise, and the night be gone? and I am full of tossings to and fro unto the dawning of the day.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־שָׁכַ֗בְתִּי וְאָמַ֗רְתִּי מָתַ֣י אָ֭קוּם וּמִדַּד־עָ֑רֶב וְשָׂבַ֖עְתִּי נְדֻדִ֣ים עֲדֵי־נָֽשֶׁף׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] When I lie down I think, ‘How long before I get up?’ The night drags on, and I toss and turn until dawn.
					\item[NRSV] When I lie down I say, ‘When shall I rise?’ But the night is long, and I am full of tossing until dawn.

			O remember that my life is wind: mine eye shall no more see good.

					\item[NRSV] “Remember that my life is a breath; my eye will never again see good.

			\label{JOB-7-8}

			The eye of him that hath seen me shall see me no more: thine eyes are upon me, and I am not.

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] The eye that now sees me will see me no longer; you will look for me, but I will be no more.
					\item[NRSV] The eye that beholds me will see me no more; while your eyes are upon me, I shall be gone.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-7-9}

			As the cloud is consumed and vanisheth away: so he that goeth down to the grave shall come up no more.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּלָ֣ה עָ֭נָן וַיֵּלַ֑ךְ כֵּ֥ן יוֹרֵ֥ד שְׁ֝א֗וֹל לֹ֣א יַעֲלֶֽה׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As the cloud is consumed and vanisheth away, So he that goeth down to Sheol shall come up no more.
					\item[NIV] As a cloud vanishes and is gone, so one who goes down to the grave does not return.
					\item[NRSV] As the cloud fades and vanishes, so those who go down to Sheol do not come up;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]

		% % ---

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-9-1}

			Then Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Then Job replied:

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 9:2} % *JOB-9-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-9-2}

			I know it is so of a truth: but how should man be just with God?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֭מְנָם יָדַ֣עְתִּי כִי־כֵ֑ן וּמַה־יִּצְדַּ֖ק אֱנ֣וֹשׁ עִם־אֵֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

	\subsubsection{Job 10} % *JOB-10
	% -----
		\label{JOB-10}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 10:1} % *JOB-10-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-10-1}

			My soul is weary of my life; I will leave my complaint upon myself; I will speak in the bitterness of my soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נָֽקְטָ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֗י בְּחַ֫יָּ֥י אֶֽעֶזְבָ֣ה עָלַ֣י שִׂיחִ֑י אֲ֝דַבְּרָה֗ בְּמַ֣ר נַפְשִֽׁי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My soul is weary of my life; I will give free course to my complaint; I will speak in the bitterness of my soul.
					\item[NIV] “I loathe my very life; therefore I will give free rein to my complaint and speak out in the bitterness of my soul.
					\item[NRSV] “I loathe my life; I will give free utterance to my complaint; I will speak in the bitterness of my soul.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 10:2} % *JOB-10-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-10-2}

			I will say unto God, Do not condemn me; shew me wherefore thou contendest with me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹמַ֣ר אֶל־אֱ֭לוֹהַּ אַל־תַּרְשִׁיעֵ֑נִי הֽ֝וֹדִיעֵ֗נִי עַ֣ל מַה־תְּרִיבֵֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I will say unto God, Do not condemn me; Show me wherefore thou contendest with me.
					\item[NIV] I say to God: Do not declare me guilty, but tell me what charges you have against me.
					\item[NRSV] I will say to God, Do not condemn me; let me know why you contend against me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 10:3} % *JOB-10-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-10-3}

			Is it good unto thee that thou shouldest oppress, that thou shouldest despise the work of thine hands, and shine upon the counsel of the wicked?

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] Will your idle talk reduce others to silence? Will no one rebuke you when you mock?
					\item[NRSV] Should your babble put others to silence, and when you mock, shall no one shame you?

			And that he would shew thee the secrets of wisdom, that they are double to that which is! Know therefore that God exacteth of thee less than thine iniquity deserveth.

					\item[BHS] \he{וְיַגֶּד־לְךָ֨ ׀ תַּֽעֲלֻמ֣וֹת חָכְמָה֮ כִּֽי־כִפְלַ֪יִם לְֽת֫וּשִׁיָּ֥ה וְדַ֡ע כִּֽי־יַשֶּׁ֥ה לְךָ֥ אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַ מֵעֲוֺנֶֽךָ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] and disclose to you the secrets of wisdom, for true wisdom has two sides. Know this: God has even forgotten some of your sin.
					\item[NRSV] and that he would tell you the secrets of wisdom! For wisdom is many-sided. Know then that God exacts of you less than your guilt deserves.

					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי־אַ֭תָּה עָמָ֣ל תִּשְׁכָּ֑ח כְּמַ֖יִם עָבְר֣וּ תִזְכֹּֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For thou shalt forget thy misery; Thou shalt remember it as waters that are passed away,
					\item[NIV] You will surely forget your trouble, recalling it only as waters gone by.
					\item[NRSV] You will forget your misery; you will remember it as waters that have passed away.

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 11:17} % *JOB-11-17
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-11-17}

			And thine age shall be clearer than the noonday: thou shalt shine forth, thou shalt be as the morning.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּֽ֭מִצָּהֳרַיִם יָק֣וּם חָ֑לֶד תָּ֝עֻ֗פָה כַּבֹּ֥קֶר תִּהְיֶֽה׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And thy life shall be clearer than the noonday; Though there be darkness, it shall be as the morning.
					\item[NIV] Life will be brighter than noonday, and darkness will become like morning.
					\item[NRSV] And your life will be brighter than the noonday; its darkness will be like the morning.

			\label{JOB-11-18}

			And thou shalt be secure, because there is hope; yea, thou shalt dig about thee, and thou shalt take thy rest in safety.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּֽ֭בָטַחְתָּ כִּי־יֵ֣שׁ תִּקְוָ֑ה וְ֝חָפַרְתָּ֗ לָבֶ֥טַח תִּשְׁכָּֽב׃}

					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] You will be secure, because there is hope; you will look about you and take your rest in safety.
					\item[NRSV] And you will have confidence, because there is hope; you will be protected and take your rest in safety.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

			Also thou shalt lie down, and none shall make thee afraid; yea, many shall make suit unto thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭רָבַצְתָּ וְאֵ֣ין מַחֲרִ֑יד וְחִלּ֖וּ פָנֶ֣יךָ רַבִּֽים׃}

					\item[NIV] You will lie down, with no one to make you afraid, and many will court your favor.
					\item[NRSV] You will lie down, and no one will make you afraid; many will entreat your favor.

			But the eyes of the wicked shall fail, and they shall not escape, and their hope shall be as the giving up of the ghost.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעֵינֵ֥י רְשָׁעִ֗ים תִּ֫כְלֶ֥ינָה וּ֭מָנוֹס אָבַ֣ד מִנְהֶ֑ם וְ֝תִקְוָתָ֗ם מַֽפַּח־נָֽפֶשׁ׃}

					\item[NRSV] But the eyes of the wicked will fail; all way of escape will be lost to them, and their hope is to breathe their last.”

			\label{JOB-12-1}

			And Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					\item[NIV] Then Job replied:
					\item[NRSV] Then Job answered:

		\paragraph{Job 12:2} % *JOB-12-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-2}

			No doubt but ye are the people, and wisdom shall die with you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֭מְנָם כִּ֣י אַתֶּם־עָ֑ם וְ֝עִמָּכֶ֗ם תָּמ֥וּת חָכְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] No doubt but ye are the people, And wisdom shall die with you.
					\item[NIV] “Doubtless you are the only people who matter, and wisdom will die with you!
					\item[NRSV] “No doubt you are the people, and wisdom will die with you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 12:3} % *JOB-12-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-3}

			But I have understanding as well as you; I am not inferior to you: yea, who knoweth not such things as these?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־לִ֤י לֵבָ֨ב ׀ כְּֽמוֹכֶ֗ם לֹא־נֹפֵ֣ל אָנֹכִ֣י מִכֶּ֑ם וְאֶת־מִי־אֵ֥ין כְּמוֹ־אֵֽלֶּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But I have understanding as well as you; I am not inferior to you: Yea, who knoweth not such things as these?
					\item[NIV] But I have a mind as well as you; I am not inferior to you. Who does not know all these things?
					\item[NRSV] But I have understanding as well as you; I am not inferior to you. Who does not know such things as these?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 12:4} % *JOB-12-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-4}

			I am as one mocked of his neighbour, who calleth upon God, and he answereth him: the just upright man is laughed to scorn.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׂחֹ֤ק לְרֵעֵ֨הוּ ׀ אֶֽהְיֶ֗ה קֹרֵ֣א לֶ֭אֱלוֹהַּ וַֽיַּעֲנֵ֑הוּ שְׂ֝ח֗וֹק צַדִּ֥יק תָּמִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I am as one that is a laughing-stock to his neighbor, I who called upon God, and he answered: The just, the perfect man is a laughing-stock.
					\item[NIV] “I have become a laughingstock to my friends, though I called on God and he answered— a mere laughingstock, though righteous and blameless!
					\item[NRSV] I am a laughingstock to my friends; I, who called upon God and he answered me, a just and blameless man, I am a laughingstock.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 12:5} % *JOB-12-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-5}

			He that is ready to slip with his feet is as a lamp despised in the thought of him that is at ease.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לַפִּ֣יד בּ֭וּז לְעַשְׁתּ֣וּת שַׁאֲנָ֑ן נָ֝כ֗וֹן לְמ֣וֹעֲדֵי רָֽגֶל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the thought of him that is at ease there is contempt for misfortune; It is ready for them whose foot slippeth.
					\item[NIV] Those who are at ease have contempt for misfortune as the fate of those whose feet are slipping.
					\item[NRSV] Those at ease have contempt for misfortune, but it is ready for those whose feet are unstable.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 12:6} % *JOB-12-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-6}

			The tabernacles of robbers prosper, and they that provoke God are secure; into whose hand God bringeth abundantly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִשְׁלָ֤יוּ אֹֽהָלִ֨ים ׀ לְשֹׁ֥דְדִ֗ים וּֽ֭בַטֻּחוֹת לְמַרְגִּ֣יזֵי אֵ֑ל לַאֲשֶׁ֤ר הֵבִ֖יא אֱל֣וֹהַּ בְּיָדֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The tents of robbers prosper, And they that provoke God are secure; Into whose hand God bringeth abundantly.
					\item[NIV] The tents of marauders are undisturbed, and those who provoke God are secure— those God has in his hand.
					\item[NRSV] The tents of robbers are at peace, and those who provoke God are secure, who bring their god in their hands.

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-7}

			But ask now the beasts, and they shall teach thee; and the fowls of the air, and they shall tell thee:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽאוּלָ֗ם שְׁאַל־נָ֣א בְהֵמ֣וֹת וְתֹרֶ֑ךָּ וְע֥וֹף הַ֝שָּׁמַ֗יִם וְיַגֶּד־לָֽךְ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] “But ask the animals, and they will teach you, or the birds in the sky, and they will tell you;
					\item[NRSV] “But ask the animals, and they will teach you; the birds of the air, and they will tell you;

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 12:8} % *JOB-12-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-8}

			Or speak to the earth, and it shall teach thee: and the fishes of the sea shall declare unto thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{א֤וֹ שִׂ֣יחַ לָאָ֣רֶץ וְתֹרֶ֑ךָּ וִֽיסַפְּר֥וּ לְ֝ךָ֗ דְּגֵ֣י הַיָּֽם׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] or speak to the earth, and it will teach you, or let the fish in the sea inform you.
					\item[NRSV] ask the plants of the earth, and they will teach you; and the fish of the sea will declare to you.

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-12-9}

			Who knoweth not in all these that the hand of the LORD hath wrought this?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭י לֹא־יָדַ֣ע בְּכָל־אֵ֑לֶּה כִּ֥י יַד־יְ֝הוָה עָ֣שְׂתָה זֹּֽאת׃}

					\item[NIV] Which of all these does not know that the hand of the LORD has done this?
					\item[NRSV] Who among all these does not know that the hand of the LORD has done this?

			In whose hand is the soul of every living thing, and the breath of all mankind.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּ֭יָדוֹ נֶ֣פֶשׁ כָּל־חָ֑י וְ֝ר֗וּחַ כָּל־בְּשַׂר־אִֽישׁ׃}

			With the ancient is wisdom; and in length of days understanding.

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] “To God belong wisdom and power; counsel and understanding are his.
					\item[NRSV] “With God are wisdom and strength; he has counsel and understanding.

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

			\label{JOB-12-14}

			Behold, he breaketh down, and it cannot be built again: he shutteth up a man, and there can be no opening.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֣ן יַ֭הֲרוֹס וְלֹ֣א יִבָּנֶ֑ה יִסְגֹּ֥ר עַל־אִ֝֗ישׁ וְלֹ֣א יִפָּתֵֽחַ׃}

					\item[NIV] What he tears down cannot be rebuilt; those he imprisons cannot be released.
					\item[NRSV] If he tears down, no one can rebuild; if he shuts someone in, no one can open up.

			Behold, he withholdeth the waters, and they dry up: also he sendeth them out, and they overturn the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֤ן יַעְצֹ֣ר בַּמַּ֣יִם וְיִבָ֑שׁוּ וִֽ֝ישַׁלְּחֵ֗ם וְיַ֖הַפְכוּ אָֽרֶץ׃}

					\item[NIV] If he holds back the waters, there is drought; if he lets them loose, they devastate the land.
					\item[NRSV] If he withholds the waters, they dry up; if he sends them out, they overwhelm the land.

			He removeth away the speech of the trusty, and taketh away the understanding of the aged.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵסִ֣יר שָׂ֭פָה לְנֶאֱמָנִ֑ים וְטַ֖עַם זְקֵנִ֣ים יִקָּֽח׃}

			He poureth contempt upon princes, and weakeneth the strength of the mighty.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שׁוֹפֵ֣ךְ בּ֭וּז עַל־נְדִיבִ֑ים וּמְזִ֖יחַ אֲפִיקִ֣ים רִפָּֽה׃}

			He discovereth deep things out of darkness, and bringeth out to light the shadow of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְגַלֶּ֣ה עֲ֭מֻקוֹת מִנִּי־חֹ֑שֶׁךְ וַיֹּצֵ֖א לָא֣וֹר צַלְמָֽוֶת׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] He reveals the deep things of darkness and brings utter darkness into the light.
					\item[NRSV] He uncovers the deeps out of darkness, and brings deep darkness to light.

			He increaseth the nations, and destroyeth them: he enlargeth the nations, and straiteneth them again.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַשְׂגִּ֣יא לַ֭גּוֹיִם וַֽיְאַבְּדֵ֑ם שֹׁטֵ֥חַ לַ֝גּוֹיִ֗ם וַיַּנְחֵֽם׃}

			He taketh away the heart of the chief of the people of the earth, and causeth them to wander in a wilderness where there is no way.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵסִ֗יר לֵ֭ב רָאשֵׁ֣י עַם־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַ֝יַּתְעֵ֗ם בְּתֹ֣הוּ לֹא־דָֽרֶךְ׃}

					\item[NIV] He deprives the leaders of the earth of their reason; he makes them wander in a trackless waste.
					\item[NRSV] He strips understanding from the leaders of the earth, and makes them wander in a pathless waste.

			They grope in the dark without light, and he maketh them to stagger like a drunken man.

			\begin{versebox}

	\subsubsection{Job 13} % *JOB-13
	% -----
		\label{JOB-13}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:1} % *JOB-13-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-1}

			Lo, mine eye hath seen all this, mine ear hath heard and understood it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֶן־כֹּ֭ל רָאֲתָ֣ה עֵינִ֑י שָֽׁמְעָ֥ה אָ֝זְנִ֗י וַתָּ֥בֶן לָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lo, mine eye hath seen all this, Mine ear hath heard and understood it.
					\item[NIV] “My eyes have seen all this, my ears have heard and understood it.
					\item[NRSV] “Look, my eye has seen all this, my ear has heard and understood it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:2} % *JOB-13-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-2}

			What ye know, the same do I know also: I am not inferior unto you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּֽ֭דַעְתְּכֶם יָדַ֣עְתִּי גַם־אָ֑נִי לֹא־נֹפֵ֖ל אָנֹכִ֣י מִכֶּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] What ye know, the same do I know also: I am not inferior unto you.
					\item[NIV] What you know, I also know; I am not inferior to you.
					\item[NRSV] What you know, I also know; I am not inferior to you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:3} % *JOB-13-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-3}

			Surely I would speak to the Almighty, and I desire to reason with God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אוּלָ֗ם אֲ֭נִי אֶל־שַׁדַּ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר וְהוֹכֵ֖חַ אֶל־אֵ֣ל אֶחְפָּֽץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Surely I would speak to the Almighty, And I desire to reason with God.
					\item[NIV] But I desire to speak to the Almighty and to argue my case with God.
					\item[NRSV] But I would speak to the Almighty, and I desire to argue my case with God.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:4} % *JOB-13-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-4}

			But ye are forgers of lies, ye are all physicians of no value.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽאוּלָ֗ם אַתֶּ֥ם טֹֽפְלֵי־שָׁ֑קֶר רֹפְאֵ֖י אֱלִ֣ל כֻּלְּכֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But ye are forgers of lies; Ye are all physicians of no value.
					\item[NIV] You, however, smear me with lies; you are worthless physicians, all of you!
					\item[NRSV] As for you, you whitewash with lies; all of you are worthless physicians.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:5} % *JOB-13-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-5}

			O that ye would altogether hold your peace! and it should be your wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִֽי־יִ֭תֵּן הַחֲרֵ֣שׁ תַּחֲרִישׁ֑וּן וּתְהִ֖י לָכֶ֣ם לְחָכְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Oh that ye would altogether hold your peace! And it would be your wisdom.
					\item[NIV] If only you would be altogether silent! For you, that would be wisdom.
					\item[NRSV] If you would only keep silent, that would be your wisdom!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:6} % *JOB-13-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-6}

			Hear now my reasoning, and hearken to the pleadings of my lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׁמְעוּ־נָ֥א תוֹכַחְתִּ֑י וְרִב֖וֹת שְׂפָתַ֣י הַקְשִֽׁיבוּ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear now my reasoning, And hearken to the pleadings of my lips.
					\item[NIV] Hear now my argument; listen to the pleas of my lips.
					\item[NRSV] Hear now my reasoning, and listen to the pleadings of my lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 13:7} % *JOB-13-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-13-7}

			Will ye speak wickedly for God? and talk deceitfully for him?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֭לְאֵל תְּדַבְּר֣וּ עַוְלָ֑ה וְ֝ל֗וֹ תְּֽדַבְּר֥וּ רְמִיָּֽה׃}

					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִ֥ישׁ שָׁכַ֗ב וְֽלֹא־יָ֫ק֥וּם עַד־בִּלְתִּ֣י שָׁ֭מַיִם לֹ֣א יָקִ֑יצוּ וְלֹֽא־יֵ֝עֹ֗רוּ מִשְּׁנָתָֽם׃}

			\label{JOB-14-13}

			O that thou wouldest hide me in the grave, that thou wouldest keep me secret, until thy wrath be past, that thou wouldest appoint me a set time, and remember me!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֤י יִתֵּ֨ן ׀ בִּשְׁא֬וֹל תַּצְפִּנֵ֗נִי תַּ֭סְתִּירֵנִי עַד־שׁ֣וּב אַפֶּ֑ךָ תָּ֤שִׁ֥ית לִ֖י חֹ֣ק וְתִזְכְּרֵֽנִי׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NRSV] O that you would hide me in Sheol, that you would conceal me until your wrath is past, that you would appoint me a set time, and remember me!

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

			\label{JOB-14-14}

			If a man die, shall he live again? all the days of my appointed time will I wait, till my change come.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־יָמ֥וּת גֶּ֗בֶר הֲיִ֫חְיֶ֥ה כָּל־יְמֵ֣י צְבָאִ֣י אֲיַחֵ֑ל עַד־בּ֝֗וֹא חֲלִיפָתִֽי׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If a man die, shall he live again? All the days of my warfare would I wait, Till my release should come.
					\item[NIV] If someone dies, will they live again? All the days of my hard service I will wait for my renewal to come.
					\item[NRSV] If mortals die, will they live again? All the days of my service I would wait until my release should come.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 14:15} % *JOB-14-15

			\label{JOB-15-11}

					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־יֶ֭עְשַׁר וְלֹא־יָק֣וּם חֵיל֑וֹ וְלֹֽא־יִטֶּ֖ה לָאָ֣רֶץ מִנְלָֽם׃}

					\item[NIV] He will no longer be rich and his wealth will not endure, nor will his possessions spread over the land.
					\item[NRSV] they will not be rich, and their wealth will not endure, nor will they strike root in the earth;

			He shall not depart out of darkness; the flame shall dry up his branches, and by the breath of his mouth shall he go away.

			\begin{versebox}

			Let not him that is deceived trust in vanity: for vanity shall be his recompence.

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] Let him not deceive himself by trusting what is worthless, for he will get nothing in return.
					\item[NRSV] Let them not trust in emptiness, deceiving themselves; for emptiness will be their recompense.

			Then Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:2} % *JOB-16-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-2}

			I have heard many such things: miserable comforters are ye all.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שָׁמַ֣עְתִּי כְאֵ֣לֶּה רַבּ֑וֹת מְנַחֲמֵ֖י עָמָ֣ל כֻּלְּכֶֽם׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I have heard many such things: Miserable comforters are ye all.
					\item[NIV] “I have heard many things like these; you are miserable comforters, all of you!
					\item[NRSV] “I have heard many such things; miserable comforters are you all.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 16:3} % *JOB-16-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-3}

			Shall vain words have an end? or what emboldeneth thee that thou answerest?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲקֵ֥ץ לְדִבְרֵי־ר֑וּחַ א֥וֹ מַה־יַּ֝מְרִֽיצְךָ֗ כִּ֣י תַעֲנֶֽה׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Shall vain words have an end? Or what provoketh thee that thou answerest?
					\item[NIV] Will your long-winded speeches never end? What ails you that you keep on arguing?
					\item[NRSV] Have windy words no limit? Or what provokes you that you keep on talking?
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:4} % *JOB-16-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-4}

			I also could speak as ye do: if your soul were in my soul’s stead, I could heap up words against you, and shake mine head at you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֤ם ׀ אָנֹכִי֮ כָּכֶ֪ם אֲדַ֫בֵּ֥רָה ל֤וּ־יֵ֪שׁ נַפְשְׁכֶ֡ם תַּ֤חַת נַפְשִׁ֗י אַחְבִּ֣ירָה עֲלֵיכֶ֣ם בְּמִלִּ֑ים וְאָנִ֥יעָה עֲ֝לֵיכֶ֗ם בְּמ֣וֹ רֹאשִֽׁי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I also could speak as ye do; If your soul were in my soul's stead, I could join words together against you, And shake my head at you.
					\item[NIV] I also could speak like you, if you were in my place; I could make fine speeches against you and shake my head at you.
					\item[NRSV] I also could talk as you do, if you were in my place; I could join words together against you, and shake my head at you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:5} % *JOB-16-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-5}

			But I would strengthen you with my mouth, and the moving of my lips should asswage your grief.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲאַמִּצְכֶ֥ם בְּמוֹ־פִ֑י וְנִ֖יד שְׂפָתַ֣י יַחְשֹֽׂךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But I would strengthen you with my mouth, And the solace of my lips would assuage your grief.
					\item[NIV] But my mouth would encourage you; comfort from my lips would bring you relief.
					\item[NRSV] I could encourage you with my mouth, and the solace of my lips would assuage your pain.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:6} % *JOB-16-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-6}

			Though I speak, my grief is not asswaged: and though I forbear, what am I eased?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִֽם־אֲ֭דַבְּרָה לֹא־יֵחָשֵׂ֣ךְ כְּאֵבִ֑י וְ֝אַחְדְּלָ֗ה מַה־מִנִּ֥י יַהֲלֹֽךְ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Though I speak, my grief is not assuaged; And though I forbear, what am I eased?
					\item[NIV] “Yet if I speak, my pain is not relieved; and if I refrain, it does not go away.
					\item[NRSV] “If I speak, my pain is not assuaged, and if I forbear, how much of it leaves me?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:7} % *JOB-16-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-7}

			But now he hath made me weary: thou hast made desolate all my company.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַךְ־עַתָּ֥ה הֶלְאָ֑נִי הֲ֝שִׁמּ֗וֹתָ כָּל־עֲדָתִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Surely, God, you have worn me out; you have devastated my entire household.
					\item[NRSV] Surely now God has worn me out; he has made desolate all my company.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:8} % *JOB-16-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-8}

			And thou hast filled me with wrinkles, which is a witness against me: and my leanness rising up in me beareth witness to my face.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽ֭תִּקְמְטֵנִי לְעֵ֣ד הָיָ֑ה וַיָּ֥קָם בִּ֥י כַ֝חֲשִׁ֗י בְּפָנַ֥י יַעֲנֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And thou hast laid fast hold on me, which is a witness against me: And my leanness riseth up against me, It testifieth to my face.
					\item[NIV] You have shriveled me up—and it has become a witness; my gauntness rises up and testifies against me.
					\item[NRSV] And he has shriveled me up, which is a witness against me; my leanness has risen up against me, and it testifies to my face.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:9} % *JOB-16-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-9}

			He teareth me in his wrath, who hateth me: he gnasheth upon me with his teeth; mine enemy sharpeneth his eyes upon me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַפּ֤וֹ טָרַ֨ף ׀ וַֽיִּשְׂטְמֵ֗נִי חָרַ֣ק עָלַ֣י בְּשִׁנָּ֑יו צָרִ֓י ׀ יִלְט֖וֹשׁ עֵינָ֣יו לִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He hath torn me in his wrath, and persecuted me; He hath gnashed upon me with his teeth: Mine adversary sharpeneth his eyes upon me.
					\item[NIV] God assails me and tears me in his anger and gnashes his teeth at me; my opponent fastens on me his piercing eyes.
					\item[NRSV] He has torn me in his wrath, and hated me; he has gnashed his teeth at me; my adversary sharpens his eyes against me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:10} % *JOB-16-10
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-10}

			They have gaped upon me with their mouth; they have smitten me upon the cheek reproachfully; they have gathered themselves together against me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פָּעֲר֬וּ עָלַ֨י ׀ בְּפִיהֶ֗ם בְּ֭חֶרְפָּה הִכּ֣וּ לְחָיָ֑י יַ֝֗חַד עָלַ֥י יִתְמַלָּאֽוּן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They have gaped upon me with their mouth; They have smitten me upon the cheek reproachfully: They gather themselves together against me.
					\item[NIV] People open their mouths to jeer at me; they strike my cheek in scorn and unite together against me.
					\item[NRSV] They have gaped at me with their mouths; they have struck me insolently on the cheek; they mass themselves together against me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 16:11} % *JOB-16-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-11}

			God hath delivered me to the ungodly, and turned me over into the hands of the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַסְגִּירֵ֣נִי אֵ֭ל אֶ֣ל עֲוִ֑יל וְעַל־יְדֵ֖י רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִרְטֵֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]

					\item[NRSV] God gives me up to the ungodly, and casts me into the hands of the wicked.

		\paragraph{Job 16:12} % *JOB-16-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-16-12}

			I was at ease, but he hath broken me asunder: he hath also taken me by my neck, and shaken me to pieces, and set me up for his mark.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שָׁ֘לֵ֤ו הָיִ֨יתִי ׀ וַֽיְפַרְפְּרֵ֗נִי וְאָחַ֣ז בְּ֭עָרְפִּי וַֽיְפַצְפְּצֵ֑נִי וַיְקִימֵ֥נִי ל֝֗וֹ לְמַטָּרָֽה׃}

					\item[NRSV] I was at ease, and he broke me in two; he seized me by the neck and dashed me to pieces; he set me up as his target;

					\item[NRSV] his archers surround me. He slashes open my kidneys, and shows no mercy; he pours out my gall on the ground.

			O that one might plead for a man with God, as a man pleadeth for his neighbour!
