	\subsubsection{Job 17} % *JOB-17
	% -----
		\label{JOB-17}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:1} % *JOB-17-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-1}

			My breath is corrupt, my days are extinct, the graves are ready for me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רוּחִ֣י חֻ֭בָּלָה יָמַ֥י נִזְעָ֗כוּ קְבָרִ֥ים לִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My spirit is consumed, my days are extinct, The grave is ready for me.
					\item[NIV] My spirit is broken, my days are cut short, the grave awaits me.
					\item[NRSV] My spirit is broken, my days are extinct, the grave is ready for me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:2} % *JOB-17-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-2}

			Are there not mockers with me? and doth not mine eye continue in their provocation?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־לֹ֣א הֲ֭תֻלִים עִמָּדִ֑י וּ֝בְהַמְּרוֹתָ֗ם תָּלַ֥ן עֵינִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Surely there are mockers with me, And mine eye dwelleth upon their provocation.
					\item[NIV] Surely mockers surround me; my eyes must dwell on their hostility.
					\item[NRSV] Surely there are mockers around me, and my eye dwells on their provocation.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:3} % *JOB-17-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-3}

			Lay down now, put me in a surety with thee; who is he that will strike hands with me?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִֽׂימָה־נָּ֭א עָרְבֵ֣נִי עִמָּ֑ךְ מִֽי ה֝֗וּא לְיָדִ֥י יִתָּקֵֽעַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Give now a pledge, be surety for me with thyself; Who is there that will strike hands with me?
					\item[NIV] “Give me, O God, the pledge you demand. Who else will put up security for me?
					\item[NRSV] “Lay down a pledge for me with yourself; who is there that will give surety for me?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:4} % *JOB-17-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-4}

			For thou hast hid their heart from understanding: therefore shalt thou not exalt them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־לִ֭בָּם צָפַ֣נְתָּ מִּשָּׂ֑כֶל עַל־כֵּ֝֗ן לֹ֣א תְרֹמֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For thou hast hid their heart from understanding: Therefore shalt thou not exalt them.
					\item[NIV] You have closed their minds to understanding; therefore you will not let them triumph.
					\item[NRSV] Since you have closed their minds to understanding, therefore you will not let them triumph.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:5} % *JOB-17-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-5}

			He that speaketh flattery to his friends, even the eyes of his children shall fail.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְ֭חֵלֶק יַגִּ֣יד רֵעִ֑ים וְעֵינֵ֖י בָנָ֣יו תִּכְלֶֽנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that denounceth his friends for a prey, Even the eyes of his children shall fail.
					\item[NIV] If anyone denounces their friends for reward, the eyes of their children will fail.
					\item[NRSV] Those who denounce friends for reward— the eyes of their children will fail.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:6} % *JOB-17-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-6}

			He hath made me also a byword of the people; and aforetime I was as a tabret.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭הִצִּגַנִי לִמְשֹׁ֣ל עַמִּ֑ים וְתֹ֖פֶת לְפָנִ֣ים אֶֽהְיֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But he hath made me a byword of the people; And they spit in my face.
					\item[NIV] “God has made me a byword to everyone, a man in whose face people spit.
					\item[NRSV] “He has made me a byword of the peoples, and I am one before whom people spit.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 17:7} % *JOB-17-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-17-7}

			Mine eye also is dim by reason of sorrow, and all my members are as a shadow.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתֵּ֣כַהּ מִכַּ֣עַשׂ עֵינִ֑י וִֽיצֻרַ֖י כַּצֵּ֣ל כֻּלָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]

			The gin shall take him by the heel, and the robber shall prevail against him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֹאחֵ֣ז בְּעָקֵ֣ב פָּ֑ח יַחֲזֵ֖ק עָלָ֣יו צַמִּֽים׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] A trap seizes him by the heel; a snare holds him fast.
					\item[NRSV] A trap seizes them by the heel; a snare lays hold of them.

			He shall neither have son nor nephew among his people, nor any remaining in his dwellings.

			They that come after him shall be astonied at his day, as they that went before were affrighted.

			Surely such are the dwellings of the wicked, and this is the place of him that knoweth not God.

			\begin{versebox}

	\subsubsection{Job 19} % *JOB-19
	% -----
		\label{JOB-19}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:1} % *JOB-19-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-1}

			Then Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then Job answered and said,
					\item[NIV] Then Job replied:
					\item[NRSV] Then Job answered:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:2} % *JOB-19-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-2}

			How long will ye vex my soul, and break me in pieces with words?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַד־אָ֭נָה תּוֹגְי֣וּן נַפְשִׁ֑י וּֽתְדַכְּאוּנַ֥נִי בְמִלִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How long will ye vex my soul, And break me in pieces with words?
					\item[NIV] “How long will you torment me and crush me with words?
					\item[NRSV] “How long will you torment me, and break me in pieces with words?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:3} % *JOB-19-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-3}

			These ten times have ye reproached me: ye are not ashamed that ye make yourselves strange to me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זֶ֤ה עֶ֣שֶׂר פְּ֭עָמִים תַּכְלִימ֑וּנִי לֹֽא־תֵ֝בֹ֗שׁוּ תַּהְכְּרוּ־לִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] These ten times have ye reproached me: Ye are not ashamed that ye deal hardly with me.
					\item[NIV] Ten times now you have reproached me; shamelessly you attack me.
					\item[NRSV] These ten times you have cast reproach upon me; are you not ashamed to wrong me?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:4} % *JOB-19-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-4}

			And be it indeed that I have erred, mine error remaineth with myself.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאַף־אָמְנָ֥ם שָׁגִ֑יתִי אִ֝תִּ֗י תָּלִ֥ין מְשׁוּגָתִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And be it indeed that I have erred, Mine error remaineth with myself.
					\item[NIV] If it is true that I have gone astray, my error remains my concern alone.
					\item[NRSV] And even if it is true that I have erred, my error remains with me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:5} % *JOB-19-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-5}

			If indeed ye will magnify yourselves against me, and plead against me my reproach:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־אָ֭מְנָם עָלַ֣י תַּגְדִּ֑ילוּ וְתוֹכִ֥יחוּ עָ֝לַ֗י חֶרְפָּתִּֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If indeed ye will magnify yourselves against me, And plead against me my reproach;
					\item[NIV] If indeed you would exalt yourselves above me and use my humiliation against me,
					\item[NRSV] If indeed you magnify yourselves against me, and make my humiliation an argument against me,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:6} % *JOB-19-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-6}

			Know now that God hath overthrown me, and hath compassed me with his net.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דְּֽעוּ־אֵ֭פוֹ כִּי־אֱל֣וֹהַּ עִוְּתָ֑נִי וּ֝מְצוּד֗וֹ עָלַ֥י הִקִּֽיף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Know now that God hath subverted me in my cause, And hath compassed me with his net.
					\item[NIV] then know that God has wronged me and drawn his net around me.
					\item[NRSV] know then that God has put me in the wrong, and closed his net around me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 19:7} % *JOB-19-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-19-7}

			Behold, I cry out of wrong, but I am not heard: I cry aloud, but there is no judgment.

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[BHS] \he{וַאֲנִ֣י יָ֭דַעְתִּי גֹּ֣אֲלִי חָ֑י וְ֝אַחֲר֗וֹן עַל־עָפָ֥ר יָקֽוּם׃}

					\item[NRSV] For I know that my Redeemer lives, and that at the last he will stand upon the earth;

			And though after my skin worms destroy this body, yet in my flesh shall I see God:

					\item[NRSV] whom I shall see on my side, and my eyes shall behold, and not another. My heart faints within me!

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] “My troubled thoughts prompt me to answer because I am greatly disturbed.
					\item[NRSV] “Pay attention! My thoughts urge me to answer, because of the agitation within me.

			Surely he shall not feel quietness in his belly, he shall not save of that which he desired.

					\item[NIV] Nothing is left for him to devour; his prosperity will not endure.

					\item[NRSV] To fill their belly to the full God will send his fierce anger into them, and rain it upon them as their food.

			\begin{versebox}

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־חֹשֶׁךְ֮ טָמ֪וּן לִצְפּ֫וּנָ֥יו תְּ֭אָכְלֵהוּ אֵ֣שׁ לֹֽא־נֻפָּ֑ח יֵ֖רַע שָׂרִ֣יד בְּאָהֳלֽוֹ׃}

					\item[NRSV] Utter darkness is laid up for their treasures; a fire fanned by no one will devour them; what is left in their tent will be consumed.

	% -----
		\label{JOB-21}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 21:1} % *JOB-21-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-21-1}

			But Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then Job answered and said,
					\item[NIV] Then Job replied:
					\item[NRSV] Then Job answered:

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 21:2} % *JOB-21-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-21-2}

			Hear diligently my speech, and let this be your consolations.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׁמְע֣וּ שָׁ֭מוֹעַ מִלָּתִ֑י וּתְהִי־זֹ֝֗את תַּנְח֥וּמֹֽתֵיכֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear diligently my speech; And let this be your consolations.
					\item[NIV] “Listen carefully to my words; let this be the consolation you give me.
					\item[NRSV] “Listen carefully to my words, and let this be your consolation.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

			\label{JOB-21-21}

			For what pleasure hath he in his house after him, when the number of his months is cut off in the midst?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י מַה־חֶפְצ֣וֹ בְּבֵית֣וֹ אַחֲרָ֑יו וּמִסְפַּ֖ר חֳדָשָׁ֣יו חֻצָּֽצוּ׃}

					\item[NIV] For what do they care about the families they leave behind when their allotted months come to an end?
					\item[NRSV] For what do they care for their household after them, when the number of their months is cut off?

			Yet shall he be brought to the grave, and shall remain in the tomb.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-21-33}

			The clods of the valley shall be sweet unto him, and every man shall draw after him, as there are innumerable before him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מָֽתְקוּ־ל֗וֹ רִגְבֵ֫י נָ֥חַל וְ֭אַחֲרָיו כָּל־אָדָ֣ם יִמְשׁ֑וֹךְ וּ֝לְפָנָ֗יו אֵ֣ין מִסְפָּֽר׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] The soil in the valley is sweet to them; everyone follows after them, and a countless throng goes before them.
					\item[NRSV] The clods of the valley are sweet to them; everyone will follow after, and those who went before are innumerable.

		\paragraph{Job 21:34} % *JOB-21-34
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-21-34}

			How then comfort ye me in vain, seeing in your answers there remaineth falsehood?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭אֵיךְ תְּנַחֲמ֣וּנִי הָ֑בֶל וּ֝תְשֽׁוּבֹתֵיכֶ֗ם נִשְׁאַר־מָֽעַל׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How then comfort ye me in vain, Seeing in your answers there remaineth only falsehood?
					\item[NIV] “So how can you console me with your nonsense? Nothing is left of your answers but falsehood!”
					\item[NRSV] How then will you comfort me with empty nothings? There is nothing left of your answers but falsehood.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

	\subsubsection{Job 22} % *JOB-22
	% -----
		\label{JOB-22}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 22:1} % *JOB-22-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-22-1}

			Then Eliphaz the Temanite answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַ֭יַּעַן אֱלִיפַ֥ז הַֽתֵּמָנִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then answered Eliphaz the Temanite, and said,
					\item[NIV] Then Eliphaz the Temanite replied:
					\item[NRSV] Then Eliphaz the Temanite answered:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 22:2} % *JOB-22-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-22-2}

			Can a man be profitable unto God, as he that is wise may be profitable unto himself?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַלְאֵ֥ל יִסְכָּן־גָּ֑בֶר כִּֽי־יִסְכֹּ֖ן עָלֵ֣ימוֹ מַשְׂכִּֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Can a man be profitable unto God? Surely he that is wise is profitable unto himself.
					\item[NIV] “Can a man be of benefit to God? Can even a wise person benefit him?
					\item[NRSV] “Can a mortal be of use to God? Can even the wisest be of service to him?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 22:3} % *JOB-22-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-22-3}

			Is it any pleasure to the Almighty, that thou art righteous? or is it gain to him, that thou makest thy ways perfect?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַחֵ֣פֶץ לְ֭שַׁדַּי כִּ֣י תִצְדָּ֑ק וְאִם־בֶּ֝֗צַע כִּֽי־תַתֵּ֥ם דְּרָכֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Is it any pleasure to the Almighty, that thou art righteous? Or is it gain to him, that thou makest thy ways perfect?
					\item[NIV] What pleasure would it give the Almighty if you were righteous? What would he gain if your ways were blameless?
					\item[NRSV] Is it any pleasure to the Almighty if you are righteous, or is it gain to him if you make your ways blameless?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 22:4} % *JOB-22-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-22-4}

			Will he reprove thee for fear of thee? will he enter with thee into judgment?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲֽ֭מִיִּרְאָ֣תְךָ יֹכִיחֶ֑ךָ יָב֥וֹא עִ֝מְּךָ֗ בַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Is it for thy fear of him that he reproveth thee, That he entereth with thee into judgment?
					\item[NIV] “Is it for your piety that he rebukes you and brings charges against you?
					\item[NRSV] Is it for your piety that he reproves you, and enters into judgment with you?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 22:5} % *JOB-22-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-22-5}

			Is not thy wickedness great? and thine iniquities infinite?

			Even to day is my complaint bitter: my stroke is heavier than my groaning.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־הַ֭יּוֹם מְרִ֣י שִׂחִ֑י יָ֝דִ֗י כָּבְדָ֥ה עַל־אַנְחָתִֽי׃}

			Oh that I knew where I might find him! that I might come even to his seat!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן יָ֭דַעְתִּי וְאֶמְצָאֵ֑הוּ אָ֝ב֗וֹא עַד־תְּכוּנָתֽוֹ׃}

			I would order my cause before him, and fill my mouth with arguments.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶעֶרְכָ֣ה לְפָנָ֣יו מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּ֝פִ֗י אֲמַלֵּ֥א תוֹכָחֽוֹת׃}

			I would know the words which he would answer me, and understand what he would say unto me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵ֭דְעָה מִלִּ֣ים יַעֲנֵ֑נִי וְ֝אָבִ֗ינָה מַה־יֹּ֥אמַר לִֽי׃}

					\item[NIV] I would find out what he would answer me, and consider what he would say to me.
					\item[NRSV] I would learn what he would answer me, and understand what he would say to me.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-6}

			Will he plead against me with his great power? No; but he would put strength in me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַבְּרָב־כֹּ֭חַ יָרִ֣יב עִמָּדִ֑י לֹ֥א אַךְ־ה֝֗וּא יָשִׂ֥ם בִּֽי׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Would he vigorously oppose me? No, he would not press charges against me.
					\item[NRSV] Would he contend with me in the greatness of his power? No; but he would give heed to me.

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 23:7} % *JOB-23-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-7}

			There the righteous might dispute with him; so should I be delivered for ever from my judge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שָׁ֗ם יָ֭שָׁר נוֹכָ֣ח עִמּ֑וֹ וַאֲפַלְּטָ֥ה לָ֝נֶ֗צַח מִשֹּׁפְטִֽי׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] There the upright can establish their innocence before him, and there I would be delivered forever from my judge.
					\item[NRSV] There an upright person could reason with him, and I should be acquitted forever by my judge.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-8}

			Behold, I go forward, but he is not there; and backward, but I cannot perceive him:

					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֤ן קֶ֣דֶם אֶהֱלֹ֣ךְ וְאֵינֶ֑נּוּ וְ֝אָח֗וֹר וְֽלֹא־אָבִ֥ין לֽוֹ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NRSV] “If I go forward, he is not there; or backward, I cannot perceive him;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 23:9} % *JOB-23-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-9}

			On the left hand, where he doth work, but I cannot behold him: he hideth himself on the right hand, that I cannot see him:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׂמֹ֣אול בַּעֲשֹׂת֣וֹ וְלֹא־אָ֑חַז יַעְטֹ֥ף יָ֝מִ֗ין וְלֹ֣א אֶרְאֶֽה׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] When he is at work in the north, I do not see him; when he turns to the south, I catch no glimpse of him.
					\item[NRSV] on the left he hides, and I cannot behold him; I turn to the right, but I cannot see him.

			But he knoweth the way that I take: when he hath tried me, I shall come forth as gold.

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[ASV] But he knoweth the way that I take; When he hath tried me, I shall come forth as gold.
					\item[NIV] But he knows the way that I take; when he has tested me, I will come forth as gold.
					\item[NRSV] But he knows the way that I take; when he has tested me, I shall come out like gold.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-11}

			My foot hath held his steps, his way have I kept, and not declined.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּ֭אֲשֻׁרוֹ אָחֲזָ֣ה רַגְלִ֑י דַּרְכּ֖וֹ שָׁמַ֣רְתִּי וְלֹא־אָֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My foot hath held fast to his steps; His way have I kept, and turned not aside.
					\item[NIV] My feet have closely followed his steps; I have kept to his way without turning aside.
					\item[NRSV] My foot has held fast to his steps; I have kept his way and have not turned aside.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-12}

			Neither have I gone back from the commandment of his lips; I have esteemed the words of his mouth more than my necessary food.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִצְוַ֣ת שְׂ֭פָתָיו וְלֹ֣א אָמִ֑ישׁ מֵ֝חֻקִּ֗י צָפַ֥נְתִּי אִמְרֵי־פִֽיו׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] I have not departed from the commands of his lips; I have treasured the words of his mouth more than my daily bread.
					\item[NRSV] I have not departed from the commandment of his lips; I have treasured in my bosom the words of his mouth.

			But he is in one mind, and who can turn him? and what his soul desireth, even that he doeth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְה֣וּא בְ֭אֶחָד וּמִ֣י יְשִׁיבֶ֑נּוּ וְנַפְשׁ֖וֹ אִוְּתָ֣ה וַיָּֽעַשׂ׃}

					\item[NIV] “But he stands alone, and who can oppose him? He does whatever he pleases.
					\item[NRSV] But he stands alone and who can dissuade him? What he desires, that he does.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-23-14}

			For he performeth the thing that is appointed for me: and many such things are with him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֭י יַשְׁלִ֣ים חֻקִּ֑י וְכָהֵ֖נָּה רַבּ֣וֹת עִמּֽוֹ׃}

					\item[NIV] He carries out his decree against me, and many such plans he still has in store.
					\item[NRSV] For he will complete what he appoints for me; and many such things are in his mind.

			Therefore am I troubled at his presence: when I consider, I am afraid of him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַל־כֵּ֭ן מִפָּנָ֣יו אֶבָּהֵ֑ל אֶ֝תְבּוֹנֵ֗ן וְאֶפְחַ֥ד מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃}

					\item[NIV] That is why I am terrified before him; when I think of all this, I fear him.
					\item[NRSV] Therefore I am terrified at his presence; when I consider, I am in dread of him.

			For God maketh my heart soft, and the Almighty troubleth me:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭אֵל הֵרַ֣ךְ לִבִּ֑י וְ֝שַׁדַּ֗י הִבְהִילָֽנִי׃}

					\item[NIV] God has made my heart faint; the Almighty has terrified me.
					\item[NRSV] God has made my heart faint; the Almighty has terrified me;

			Because I was not cut off before the darkness, neither hath he covered the darkness from my face.

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] Yet I am not silenced by the darkness, by the thick darkness that covers my face.

	\subsubsection{Job 24} % *JOB-24
	% -----
		\label{JOB-24}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 24:1} % *JOB-24-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-1}

			Why, seeing times are not hidden from the Almighty, do they that know him not see his days?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַדּ֗וּעַ מִ֭שַּׁדַּי לֹא־נִצְפְּנ֣וּ עִתִּ֑ים וְ֝יֹדְעָ֗יו לֹא־חָ֥זוּ יָמָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Why are times not laid up by the Almighty? And why do not they that know him see his days?
					\item[NIV] “Why does the Almighty not set times for judgment? Why must those who know him look in vain for such days?
					\item[NRSV] “Why are times not kept by the Almighty, and why do those who know him never see his days?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 24:2} % *JOB-24-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-2}

			Some remove the landmarks; they violently take away flocks, and feed thereof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גְּבֻל֥וֹת יַשִּׂ֑יגוּ עֵ֥דֶר גָּ֝זְל֗וּ וַיִּרְעֽוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There are that remove the landmarks; They violently take away flocks, and feed them.
					\item[NIV] There are those who move boundary stones; they pasture flocks they have stolen.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked remove landmarks; they seize flocks and pasture them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-3}

			They drive away the ass of the fatherless, they take the widow’s ox for a pledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֲמ֣וֹר יְתוֹמִ֣ים יִנְהָ֑גוּ יַ֝חְבְּל֗וּ שׁ֣וֹר אַלְמָנָֽה׃}

					\item[NIV] They drive away the orphan’s donkey and take the widow’s ox in pledge.
					\item[NRSV] They drive away the donkey of the orphan; they take the widow’s ox for a pledge.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-4}

			They turn the needy out of the way: the poor of the earth hide themselves together.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַטּ֣וּ אֶבְיוֹנִ֣ים מִדָּ֑רֶךְ יַ֥חַד חֻ֝בְּא֗וּ עֲנִיֵּי־אָֽרֶץ׃}

					\item[NIV] They thrust the needy from the path and force all the poor of the land into hiding.
					\item[NRSV] They thrust the needy off the road; the poor of the earth all hide themselves.

		\paragraph{Job 24:5} % *JOB-24-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-5}

			Behold, as wild asses in the desert, go they forth to their work; rising betimes for a prey: the wilderness yieldeth food for them and for their children.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֤ן פְּרָאִ֨ים ׀ בַּֽמִּדְבָּ֗ר יָצְא֣וּ בְּ֭פָעֳלָם מְשַׁחֲרֵ֣י לַטָּ֑רֶף עֲרָבָ֥ה ל֥וֹ לֶ֝֗חֶם לַנְּעָרִֽים׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Like wild donkeys in the desert, the poor go about their labor of foraging food; the wasteland provides food for their children.
					\item[NRSV] Like wild asses in the desert they go out to their toil, scavenging in the wasteland food for their young.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-6}

			They reap every one his corn in the field: and they gather the vintage of the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּ֭שָּׂדֶה בְּלִיל֣וֹ יִקְצ֑וֹרוּ וְכֶ֖רֶם רָשָׁ֣ע יְלַקֵּֽשׁוּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] They gather fodder in the fields and glean in the vineyards of the wicked.
					\item[NRSV] They reap in a field not their own and they glean in the vineyard of the wicked.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-7}

			They cause the naked to lodge without clothing, that they have no covering in the cold.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָר֣וֹם יָ֭לִינוּ מִבְּלִ֣י לְב֑וּשׁ וְאֵ֥ין כְּ֝ס֗וּת בַּקָּרָֽה׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Lacking clothes, they spend the night naked; they have nothing to cover themselves in the cold.
					\item[NRSV] They lie all night naked, without clothing, and have no covering in the cold.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-8}

			They are wet with the showers of the mountains, and embrace the rock for want of a shelter.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִזֶּ֣רֶם הָרִ֣ים יִרְטָ֑בוּ וּֽמִבְּלִ֥י מַ֝חְסֶ֗ה חִבְּקוּ־צֽוּר׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] They are drenched by mountain rains and hug the rocks for lack of shelter.
					\item[NRSV] They are wet with the rain of the mountains, and cling to the rock for want of shelter.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-9}

			They pluck the fatherless from the breast, and take a pledge of the poor.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִ֭גְזְלוּ מִשֹּׁ֣ד יָת֑וֹם וְֽעַל־עָנִ֥י יַחְבֹּֽלוּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] The fatherless child is snatched from the breast; the infant of the poor is seized for a debt.
					\item[NRSV] “There are those who snatch the orphan child from the breast, and take as a pledge the infant of the poor.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-10}

			They cause him to go naked without clothing, and they take away the sheaf from the hungry;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָר֣וֹם הִ֭לְּכוּ בְּלִ֣י לְב֑וּשׁ וּ֝רְעֵבִ֗ים נָ֣שְׂאוּ עֹֽמֶר׃}

					\item[NIV] Lacking clothes, they go about naked; they carry the sheaves, but still go hungry.
					\item[NRSV] They go about naked, without clothing; though hungry, they carry the sheaves;

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-11}

			Which make oil within their walls, and tread their winepresses, and suffer thirst.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּין־שׁוּרֹתָ֥ם יַצְהִ֑ירוּ יְקָבִ֥ים דָּ֝רְכ֗וּ וַיִּצְמָֽאוּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] They crush olives among the terraces; they tread the winepresses, yet suffer thirst.
					\item[NRSV] between their terraces they press out oil; they tread the wine presses, but suffer thirst.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 24:12} % *JOB-24-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-12}

			Men groan from out of the city, and the soul of the wounded crieth out: yet God layeth not folly to them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵ֘עִ֤יר מְתִ֨ים ׀ יִנְאָ֗קוּ וְנֶֽפֶשׁ־חֲלָלִ֥ים תְּשַׁוֵּ֑עַ וֶ֝אֱל֗וֹהַּ לֹא־יָשִׂ֥ים תִּפְלָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] From out of the populous city men groan, And the soul of the wounded crieth out: Yet God regardeth not the folly.
					\item[NIV] The groans of the dying rise from the city, and the souls of the wounded cry out for help. But God charges no one with wrongdoing.
					\item[NRSV] From the city the dying groan, and the throat of the wounded cries for help; yet God pays no attention to their prayer.

			In the dark they dig through houses, which they had marked for themselves in the daytime: they know not the light.

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] For all of them, midnight is their morning; they make friends with the terrors of darkness.

					\item[NRSV] “Swift are they on the face of the waters; their portion in the land is cursed; no treader turns toward their vineyards.

			The womb shall forget him; the worm shall feed sweetly on him; he shall be no more remembered; and wickedness shall be broken as a tree.

			\begin{versebox}

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-24-24}

			They are exalted for a little while, but are gone and brought low; they are taken out of the way as all other, and cut off as the tops of the ears of corn.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ר֤וֹמּוּ מְּעַ֨ט ׀ וְֽאֵינֶ֗נּוּ וְֽהֻמְּכ֗וּ כַּכֹּ֥ל יִקָּפְצ֑וּן וּכְרֹ֖אשׁ שִׁבֹּ֣לֶת יִמָּֽלוּ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[ASV] They are exalted; yet a little while, and they are gone; Yea, they are brought low, they are taken out of the way as all others, And are cut off as the tops of the ears of grain.
					\item[NIV] For a little while they are exalted, and then they are gone; they are brought low and gathered up like all others; they are cut off like heads of grain.
					\item[NRSV] They are exalted a little while, and then are gone; they wither and fade like the mallow; they are cut off like the heads of grain.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

			And if it be not so now, who will make me a liar, and make my speech nothing worth?

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-25-4}

			How then can man be justified with God? or how can he be clean that is born of a woman?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמַה־יִּצְדַּ֣ק אֱנ֣וֹשׁ עִם־אֵ֑ל וּמַה־יִּ֝זְכֶּ֗ה יְל֣וּד אִשָּֽׁה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How then can man be just with God? Or how can he be clean that is born of a woman?
					\item[NIV] How then can a mortal be righteous before God? How can one born of woman be pure?
					\item[NRSV] How then can a mortal be righteous before God? How can one born of woman be pure?

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 25:5} % *JOB-25-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-25-5}

			Behold even to the moon, and it shineth not; yea, the stars are not pure in his sight.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֣ן עַד־יָ֭רֵחַ וְלֹ֣א יַאֲהִ֑יל וְ֝כוֹכָבִ֗ים לֹא־זַכּ֥וּ בְעֵינָֽיו׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, even the moon hath no brightness, And the stars are not pure in his sight:
					\item[NIV] If even the moon is not bright and the stars are not pure in his eyes,
					\item[NRSV] If even the moon is not bright and the stars are not pure in his sight,

			How much less man, that is a worm? and the son of man, which is a worm?

					\item[NIV] how much less a mortal, who is but a maggot— a human being, who is only a worm!”
					\item[NRSV] how much less a mortal, who is a maggot, and a human being, who is a worm!”

			\label{JOB-26-1}

			But Job answered and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					\item[NRSV] Then Job answered:

		\paragraph{Job 26:2} % *JOB-26-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-26-2}

			How hast thou helped him that is without power? how savest thou the arm that hath no strength?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֶה־עָזַ֥רְתָּ לְלֹא־כֹ֑חַ ה֝וֹשַׁ֗עְתָּ זְר֣וֹעַ לֹא־עֹֽז׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How hast thou helped him that is without power! How hast thou saved the arm that hath no strength!
					\item[NIV] “How you have helped the powerless! How you have saved the arm that is feeble!
					\item[NRSV] “How you have helped one who has no power! How you have assisted the arm that has no strength!

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-26-3}

			How hast thou counselled him that hath no wisdom? and how hast thou plentifully declared the thing as it is?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַה־יָּ֭עַצְתָּ לְלֹ֣א חָכְמָ֑ה וְ֝תוּשִׁיָּ֗ה לָרֹ֥ב הוֹדָֽעְתָּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] What advice you have offered to one without wisdom! And what great insight you have displayed!
					\item[NRSV] How you have counseled one who has no wisdom, and given much good advice!

			Dead things are formed from under the waters, and the inhabitants thereof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָרְפָאִ֥ים יְחוֹלָ֑לוּ מִתַּ֥חַת מַ֝֗יִם וְשֹׁכְנֵיהֶֽם׃}

			Hell is naked before him, and destruction hath no covering.

			He bindeth up the waters in his thick clouds; and the cloud is not rent under them.

			He holdeth back the face of his throne, and spreadeth his cloud upon it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְאַחֵ֥ז פְּנֵי־כִסֵּ֑ה פַּרְשֵׁ֖ז עָלָ֣יו עֲנָנֽוֹ׃}

			\label{JOB-26-10}

			He hath compassed the waters with bounds, until the day and night come to an end.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֹֽק־חָ֭ג עַל־פְּנֵי־מָ֑יִם עַד־תַּכְלִ֖ית א֣וֹר עִם־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃}

					\item[NIV] He marks out the horizon on the face of the waters for a boundary between light and darkness.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-26-11}

			The pillars of heaven tremble and are astonished at his reproof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַמּוּדֵ֣י שָׁמַ֣יִם יְרוֹפָ֑פוּ וְ֝יִתְמְה֗וּ מִגַּעֲרָתֽוֹ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] The pillars of the heavens quake, aghast at his rebuke.
					\item[NRSV] The pillars of heaven tremble, and are astounded at his rebuke.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-26-12}

			He divideth the sea with his power, and by his understanding he smiteth through the proud.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭כֹחוֹ רָגַ֣ע הַיָּ֑ם וּ֝בִתְבוּנָת֗וֹ מָ֣חַץ רָֽהַב׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] By his power he churned up the sea; by his wisdom he cut Rahab to pieces.
					\item[NRSV] By his power he stilled the Sea; by his understanding he struck down Rahab.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 26:13} % *JOB-26-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-26-13}

			By his spirit he hath garnished the heavens; his hand hath formed the crooked serpent.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭רוּחוֹ שָׁמַ֣יִם שִׁפְרָ֑ה חֹֽלֲלָ֥ה יָ֝ד֗וֹ נָחָ֥שׁ בָּרִֽיחַ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By his Spirit the heavens are garnished; His hand hath pierced the swift serpent.
					\item[NIV] By his breath the skies became fair; his hand pierced the gliding serpent.
					\item[NRSV] By his wind the heavens were made fair; his hand pierced the fleeing serpent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 26:14} % *JOB-26-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-26-14}

			Lo, these are parts of his ways: but how little a portion is heard of him? but the thunder of his power who can understand?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֶן־אֵ֤לֶּה ׀ קְצ֬וֹת דְּרָכָ֗יו וּמַה־שֵּׁ֣מֶץ דָּ֭בָר נִשְׁמַע־בּ֑וֹ וְרַ֥עַם גְּ֝בוּרוֹתָ֗יו מִ֣י יִתְבּוֹנָֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lo, these are but the outskirts of his ways: And how small a whisper do we hear of him! But the thunder of his power who can understand?
					\item[NIV] And these are but the outer fringe of his works; how faint the whisper we hear of him! Who then can understand the thunder of his power?”
					\item[NRSV] These are indeed but the outskirts of his ways; and how small a whisper do we hear of him! But the thunder of his power who can understand?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			Moreover Job continued his parable, and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣סֶף אִ֭יּוֹב שְׂאֵ֥ת מְשָׁל֗וֹ וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 27:2} % *JOB-27-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-27-2}

			As God liveth, who hath taken away my judgment; and the Almighty, who hath vexed my soul;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַי־אֵ֭ל הֵסִ֣יר מִשְׁפָּטִ֑י וְ֝שַׁדַּ֗י הֵמַ֥ר נַפְשִֽׁי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As God liveth, who hath taken away my right, And the Almighty, who hath vexed my soul:
					\item[NIV] “As surely as God lives, who has denied me justice, the Almighty, who has made my life bitter,
					\item[NRSV] “As God lives, who has taken away my right, and the Almighty, who has made my soul bitter,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 27:3} % *JOB-27-3

			The east wind carrieth him away, and he departeth: and as a storm hurleth him out of his place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִשָּׂאֵ֣הוּ קָדִ֣ים וְיֵלַ֑ךְ וִֽ֝ישָׂעֲרֵ֗הוּ מִמְּקֹמֽוֹ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The east wind carrieth him away, and he departeth; And it sweepeth him out of his place.
					\item[NIV] The east wind carries him off, and he is gone; it sweeps him out of his place.
					\item[NRSV] The east wind lifts them up and they are gone; it sweeps them out of their place.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 27:22} % *JOB-27-22
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-27-22}

			For God shall cast upon him, and not spare: he would fain flee out of his hand.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְיַשְׁלֵ֣ךְ עָ֭לָיו וְלֹ֣א יַחְמֹ֑ל מִ֝יָּד֗וֹ בָּר֥וֹחַ יִבְרָֽח׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For God shall hurl at him, and not spare: He would fain flee out of his hand.
					\item[NIV] It hurls itself against him without mercy as he flees headlong from its power.
					\item[NRSV] It hurls at them without pity; they flee from its power in headlong flight.

		% % ---

			Men shall clap their hands at him, and shall hiss him out of his place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִשְׂפֹּ֣ק עָלֵ֣ימוֹ כַפֵּ֑ימוֹ וְיִשְׁרֹ֥ק עָ֝לָ֗יו מִמְּקֹמֽוֹ׃}

					\item[NIV] It claps its hands in derision and hisses him out of his place.”
					\item[NRSV] It claps its hands at them, and hisses at them from its place.

	\subsubsection{Job 28} % *JOB-28

		\label{JOB-28}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 28:1} % *JOB-28-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-1}

			Surely there is a vein for the silver, and a place for gold where they fine it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י יֵ֣שׁ לַכֶּ֣סֶף מוֹצָ֑א וּ֝מָק֗וֹם לַזָּהָ֥ב יָזֹֽקּוּ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Surely there is a mine for silver, And a place for gold which they refine.
					\item[NIV] There is a mine for silver and a place where gold is refined.
					\item[NRSV] “Surely there is a mine for silver, and a place for gold to be refined.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-2}

			Iron is taken out of the earth, and brass is molten out of the stone.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּ֭רְזֶל מֵעָפָ֣ר יֻקָּ֑ח וְ֝אֶ֗בֶן יָצ֥וּק נְחוּשָֽׁה׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Iron is taken from the earth, and copper is smelted from ore.
					\item[NRSV] Iron is taken out of the earth, and copper is smelted from ore.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-3}

			He setteth an end to darkness, and searcheth out all perfection: the stones of darkness, and the shadow of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קֵ֤ץ ׀ שָׂ֤ם לַחֹ֗שֶׁךְ וּֽלְכָל־תַּ֭כְלִית ה֣וּא חוֹקֵ֑ר אֶ֖בֶן אֹ֣פֶל וְצַלְמָֽוֶת׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Man setteth an end to darkness, And searcheth out, to the furthest bound, The stones of obscurity and of thick darkness.
					\item[NIV] Mortals put an end to the darkness; they search out the farthest recesses for ore in the blackest darkness.
					\item[NRSV] Miners put an end to darkness, and search out to the farthest bound the ore in gloom and deep darkness.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 28:4} % *JOB-28-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-4}

			The flood breaketh out from the inhabitant; even the waters forgotten of the foot: they are dried up, they are gone away from men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פָּ֤רַץ נַ֨חַל ׀ מֵֽעִם־גָּ֗ר הַֽנִּשְׁכָּחִ֥ים מִנִּי־רָ֑גֶל דַּ֖לּוּ מֵאֱנ֣וֹשׁ נָֽעוּ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He breaketh open a shaft away from where men sojourn; They are forgotten of the foot; They hang afar from men, they swing to and fro.
					\item[NIV] Far from human dwellings they cut a shaft, in places untouched by human feet; far from other people they dangle and sway.
					\item[NRSV] They open shafts in a valley away from human habitation; they are forgotten by travelers, they sway suspended, remote from people.

			\begin{versebox}

			The lion’s whelps have not trodden it, nor the fierce lion passed by it.

			He putteth forth his hand upon the rock; he overturneth the mountains by the roots.

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NIV] People assault the flinty rock with their hands and lay bare the roots of the mountains.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-10}

			He cutteth out rivers among the rocks; and his eye seeth every precious thing.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּ֭צּוּרוֹת יְאֹרִ֣ים בִּקֵּ֑עַ וְכָל־יְ֝קָ֗ר רָאֲתָ֥ה עֵינֽוֹ׃}

					\item[NIV] They tunnel through the rock; their eyes see all its treasures.
					\item[NRSV] They cut out channels in the rocks, and their eyes see every precious thing.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 28:11} % *JOB-28-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-11}

			He bindeth the floods from overflowing; and the thing that is hid bringeth he forth to light.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭בְּכִי נְהָר֣וֹת חִבֵּ֑שׁ וְ֝תַעֲלֻמָ֗הּ יֹ֣צִא אֽוֹר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] They search the sources of the rivers and bring hidden things to light.
					\item[NRSV] The sources of the rivers they probe; hidden things they bring to light.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-12}

			But where shall wisdom be found? and where is the place of understanding?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭הַחָכְמָה מֵאַ֣יִן תִּמָּצֵ֑א וְאֵ֥י זֶ֝ה מְק֣וֹם בִּינָֽה׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] But where can wisdom be found? Where does understanding dwell?
					\item[NRSV] “But where shall wisdom be found? And where is the place of understanding?
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 28:13} % *JOB-28-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-13}

			Man knoweth not the price thereof; neither is it found in the land of the living.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יָדַ֣ע אֱנ֣וֹשׁ עֶרְכָּ֑הּ וְלֹ֥א תִ֝מָּצֵ֗א בְּאֶ֣רֶץ הַֽחַיִּֽים׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Man knoweth not the price thereof; Neither is it found in the land of the living.
					\item[NIV] No mortal comprehends its worth; it cannot be found in the land of the living.
					\item[NRSV] Mortals do not know the way to it, and it is not found in the land of the living.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 28:14} % *JOB-28-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-14}

			The depth saith, It is not in me: and the sea saith, It is not with me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תְּה֣וֹם אָ֭מַר לֹ֣א בִי־הִ֑יא וְיָ֥ם אָ֝מַ֗ר אֵ֣ין עִמָּדִֽי׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] The deep says, “It is not in me"; the sea says, “It is not with me.”
					\item[NRSV] The deep says, ‘It is not in me,’ and the sea says, ‘It is not with me.’

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-15}

			It cannot be gotten for gold, neither shall silver be weighed for the price thereof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יֻתַּ֣ן סְג֣וֹר תַּחְתֶּ֑יהָ וְלֹ֥א יִ֝שָּׁקֵ֗ל כֶּ֣סֶף מְחִירָֽהּ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] It cannot be bought with the finest gold, nor can its price be weighed out in silver.
					\item[NRSV] It cannot be gotten for gold, and silver cannot be weighed out as its price.

					% % \item[OTXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 28:16} % *JOB-28-16
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-28-16}

			It cannot be valued with the gold of Ophir, with the precious onyx, or the sapphire.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־תְ֭סֻלֶּה בְּכֶ֣תֶם אוֹפִ֑יר בְּשֹׁ֖הַם יָקָ֣ר וְסַפִּֽיר׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It cannot be valued with the gold of Ophir, With the precious onyx, or the sapphire.
					\item[NIV] It cannot be bought with the gold of Ophir, with precious onyx or lapis lazuli.
					\item[NRSV] It cannot be valued in the gold of Ophir, in precious onyx or sapphire.

			The gold and the crystal cannot equal it: and the exchange of it shall not be for jewels of fine gold.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יַעַרְכֶ֣נָּה זָ֭הָב וּזְכוֹכִ֑ית וּתְמ֖וּרָתָ֣הּ כְּלִי־פָֽז׃}

					\item[NRSV] Gold and glass cannot equal it, nor can it be exchanged for jewels of fine gold.

			And unto man he said, Behold, the fear of the LORD, that is wisdom; and to depart from evil is understanding.

					\item[NRSV] And he said to humankind, ‘Truly, the fear of the Lord, that is wisdom; and to depart from evil is understanding.’”

	\subsubsection{Job 29} % *JOB-29

		\label{JOB-29}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 29:1} % *JOB-29-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-1}

			Moreover Job continued his parable, and said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣סֶף אִ֭יּוֹב שְׂאֵ֥ת מְשָׁל֗וֹ וַיֹּאמַֽר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Job continued his discourse:
					\item[NRSV] Job again took up his discourse and said:

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-2}

			Oh that I were as in months past, as in the days when God preserved me;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִֽי־יִתְּנֵ֥נִי כְיַרְחֵי־קֶ֑דֶם כִּ֝ימֵ֗י אֱל֣וֹהַּ יִשְׁמְרֵֽנִי׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Oh that I were as in the months of old, As in the days when God watched over me;
					\item[NIV] “How I long for the months gone by, for the days when God watched over me,
					\item[NRSV] “O that I were as in the months of old, as in the days when God watched over me;

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-3}

			When his candle shined upon my head, and when by his light I walked through darkness;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּהִלּ֣וֹ נֵ֭רוֹ עֲלֵ֣י רֹאשִׁ֑י לְ֝אוֹרוֹ אֵ֣לֶךְ חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When his lamp shined upon my head, And by his light I walked through darkness;
					\item[NIV] when his lamp shone on my head and by his light I walked through darkness!
					\item[NRSV] when his lamp shone over my head, and by his light I walked through darkness;
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-4}

			As I was in the days of my youth, when the secret of God was upon my tabernacle;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר הָ֭יִיתִי בִּימֵ֣י חָרְפִּ֑י בְּס֥וֹד אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ עֲלֵ֣י אָהֳלִֽי׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Oh, for the days when I was in my prime, when God’s intimate friendship blessed my house,
					\item[NRSV] when I was in my prime, when the friendship of God was upon my tent;

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-5}

			When the Almighty was yet with me, when my children were about me;

					\item[BHS] \he{בְּע֣וֹד שַׁ֭דַּי עִמָּדִ֑י סְבִ֖יבוֹתַ֣י נְעָרָֽי׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] when the Almighty was still with me and my children were around me,
					\item[NRSV] when the Almighty was still with me, when my children were around me;

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-6}

			When I washed my steps with butter, and the rock poured me out rivers of oil;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּרְחֹ֣ץ הֲלִיכַ֣י בְּחֵמָ֑ה וְצ֥וּר יָצ֥וּק עִ֝מָּדִ֗י פַּלְגֵי־שָֽׁמֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 29:25} % *JOB-29-25
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-29-25}

			I chose out their way, and sat chief, and dwelt as a king in the army, as one that comforteth the mourners.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶֽבֲחַ֣ר דַּרְכָּם֮ וְאֵשֵׁ֪ב רֹ֥אשׁ וְ֭אֶשְׁכּוֹן כְּמֶ֣לֶךְ בַּגְּד֑וּד כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר אֲבֵלִ֣ים יְנַחֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I chose out their way, and sat as chief, And dwelt as a king in the army, As one that comforteth the mourners.
					\item[NIV] I chose the way for them and sat as their chief; I dwelt as a king among his troops; I was like one who comforts mourners.
					\item[NRSV] I chose their way, and sat as chief, and I lived like a king among his troops, like one who comforts mourners.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Job 30} % *JOB-30
	% -----
		\label{JOB-30}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:1} % *JOB-30-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-1}

			But now they that are younger than I have me in derision, whose fathers I would have disdained to have set with the dogs of my flock.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֤ה ׀ שָֽׂחֲק֣וּ עָלַי֮ צְעִירִ֥ים מִמֶּ֗נִּי לְיָ֫מִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־מָאַ֥סְתִּי אֲבוֹתָ֑ם לָ֝שִׁ֗ית עִם־כַּלְבֵ֥י צֹאנִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But now they that are younger than I have me in derision, Whose fathers I disdained to set with the dogs of my flock.
					\item[NIV] “But now they mock me, men younger than I, whose fathers I would have disdained to put with my sheep dogs.
					\item[NRSV] “But now they make sport of me, those who are younger than I, whose fathers I would have disdained to set with the dogs of my flock.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:2} % *JOB-30-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-2}

			Yea, whereto might the strength of their hands profit me, in whom old age was perished?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־כֹּ֣חַ יְ֭דֵיהֶם לָ֣מָּה לִּ֑י עָ֝לֵ֗ימוֹ אָ֣בַד כָּֽלַח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, the strength of their hands, whereto should it profit me? Men in whom ripe age is perished.
					\item[NIV] Of what use was the strength of their hands to me, since their vigor had gone from them?
					\item[NRSV] What could I gain from the strength of their hands? All their vigor is gone.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:3} % *JOB-30-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-3}

			For want and famine they were solitary; fleeing into the wilderness in former time desolate and waste.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּחֶ֥סֶר וּבְכָפָ֗ן גַּ֫לְמ֥וּד הַֽעֹרְקִ֥ים צִיָּ֑ה אֶ֝֗מֶשׁ שׁוֹאָ֥ה וּמְשֹׁאָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They are gaunt with want and famine; They gnaw the dry ground, in the gloom of wasteness and desolation.
					\item[NIV] Haggard from want and hunger, they roamed the parched land in desolate wastelands at night.
					\item[NRSV] Through want and hard hunger they gnaw the dry and desolate ground,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:4} % *JOB-30-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-4}

			Who cut up mallows by the bushes, and juniper roots for their meat.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַקֹּטְפִ֣ים מַלּ֣וּחַ עֲלֵי־שִׂ֑יחַ וְשֹׁ֖רֶשׁ רְתָמִ֣ים לַחְמָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They pluck salt-wort by the bushes; And the roots of the broom are their food.
					\item[NIV] In the brush they gathered salt herbs, and their food was the root of the broom bush.
					\item[NRSV] they pick mallow and the leaves of bushes, and to warm themselves the roots of broom.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:5} % *JOB-30-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-5}

			They were driven forth from among men, (they cried after them as after a thief;)

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִן־גֵּ֥ו יְגֹרָ֑שׁוּ יָרִ֥יעוּ עָ֝לֵ֗ימוֹ כַּגַּנָּֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They are driven forth from the midst of men; They cry after them as after a thief;
					\item[NIV] They were banished from human society, shouted at as if they were thieves.
					\item[NRSV] They are driven out from society; people shout after them as after a thief.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:6} % *JOB-30-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-6}

			To dwell in the cliffs of the valleys, in caves of the earth, and in the rocks.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּעֲר֣וּץ נְחָלִ֣ים לִשְׁכֹּ֑ן חֹרֵ֖י עָפָ֣ר וְכֵפִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So that they dwell in frightful valleys, In holes of the earth and of the rocks.
					\item[NIV] They were forced to live in the dry stream beds, among the rocks and in holes in the ground.
					\item[NRSV] In the gullies of wadis they must live, in holes in the ground, and in the rocks.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:7} % *JOB-30-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-7}

			Among the bushes they brayed; under the nettles they were gathered together.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּין־שִׂיחִ֥ים יִנְהָ֑קוּ תַּ֖חַת חָר֣וּל יְסֻפָּֽחוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Among the bushes they bray; Under the nettles they are gathered together.
					\item[NIV] They brayed among the bushes and huddled in the undergrowth.
					\item[NRSV] Among the bushes they bray; under the nettles they huddle together.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 30:8} % *JOB-30-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-8}

			They were children of fools, yea, children of base men: they were viler than the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּֽנֵי־נָ֭בָל גַּם־בְּנֵ֣י בְלִי־שֵׁ֑ם נִ֝כְּא֗וּ מִן־הָאָֽרֶץ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They are children of fools, yea, children of base men; They were scouraged out of the land.
					\item[NIV] A base and nameless brood, they were driven out of the land.
					\item[NRSV] A senseless, disreputable brood, they have been whipped out of the land.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:9} % *JOB-30-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-9}

			And now am I their song, yea, I am their byword.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭עַתָּה נְגִינָתָ֣ם הָיִ֑יתִי וָאֱהִ֖י לָהֶ֣ם לְמִלָּֽה׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And now I am become their song, Yea, I am a byword unto them.
					\item[NIV] “And now those young men mock me in song; I have become a byword among them.
					\item[NRSV] “And now they mock me in song; I am a byword to them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:10} % *JOB-30-10
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-10}

			They abhor me, they flee far from me, and spare not to spit in my face.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תִּֽ֭עֲבוּנִי רָ֣חֲקוּ מֶ֑נִּי וּ֝מִפָּנַ֗י לֹא־חָ֥שְׂכוּ רֹֽק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They abhor me, they stand aloof from me, And spare not to spit in my face.
					\item[NIV] They detest me and keep their distance; they do not hesitate to spit in my face.
					\item[NRSV] They abhor me, they keep aloof from me; they do not hesitate to spit at the sight of me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:11} % *JOB-30-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-11}

			Because he hath loosed my cord, and afflicted me, they have also let loose the bridle before me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִתְרִ֣י פִ֭תַּח וַיְעַנֵּ֑נִי וְ֝רֶ֗סֶן מִפָּנַ֥י שִׁלֵּֽחוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For he hath loosed his cord, and afflicted me; And they have cast off the bridle before me.
					\item[NIV] Now that God has unstrung my bow and afflicted me, they throw off restraint in my presence.
					\item[NRSV] Because God has loosed my bowstring and humbled me, they have cast off restraint in my presence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:12} % *JOB-30-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-12}

			Upon my right hand rise the youth; they push away my feet, and they raise up against me the ways of their destruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַל־יָמִין֮ פִּרְחַ֪ח יָ֫ק֥וּמוּ רַגְלַ֥י שִׁלֵּ֑חוּ וַיָּסֹ֥לּוּ עָ֝לַ֗י אָרְח֥וֹת אֵידָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Upon my right hand rise the rabble; They thrust aside my feet, And they cast up against me their ways of destruction.
					\item[NIV] On my right the tribe attacks; they lay snares for my feet, they build their siege ramps against me.
					\item[NRSV] On my right hand the rabble rise up; they send me sprawling, and build roads for my ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:13} % *JOB-30-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-13}

			They mar my path, they set forward my calamity, they have no helper.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נָתְס֗וּ נְֽתִיבָ֫תִ֥י לְהַוָּתִ֥י יֹעִ֑ילוּ לֹ֖א עֹזֵ֣ר לָֽמוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They mar my path, They set forward my calamity, Even men that have no helper.
					\item[NIV] They break up my road; they succeed in destroying me. ‘No one can help him,’ they say.
					\item[NRSV] They break up my path, they promote my calamity; no one restrains them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:14} % *JOB-30-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-14}

			They came upon me as a wide breaking in of waters: in the desolation they rolled themselves upon me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּפֶ֣רֶץ רָחָ֣ב יֶאֱתָ֑יוּ תַּ֥חַת שֹׁ֝אָ֗ה הִתְגַּלְגָּֽלוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As through a wide breach they come: In the midst of the ruin they roll themselves upon me.
					\item[NIV] They advance as through a gaping breach; amid the ruins they come rolling in.
					\item[NRSV] As through a wide breach they come; amid the crash they roll on.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:15} % *JOB-30-15
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-15}

			Terrors are turned upon me: they pursue my soul as the wind: and my welfare passeth away as a cloud.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָהְפַּ֥ךְ עָלַ֗י בַּלָּ֫ה֥וֹת תִּרְדֹּ֣ף כָּ֭רוּחַ נְדִבָתִ֑י וּ֝כְעָ֗ב עָבְרָ֥ה יְשֻׁעָתִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Terrors are turned upon me; They chase mine honor as the wind; And my welfare is passed away as a cloud.
					\item[NIV] Terrors overwhelm me; my dignity is driven away as by the wind, my safety vanishes like a cloud.
					\item[NRSV] Terrors are turned upon me; my honor is pursued as by the wind, and my prosperity has passed away like a cloud.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:16} % *JOB-30-16
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-16}

			And now my soul is poured out upon me; the days of affliction have taken hold upon me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֗ה עָ֭לַי תִּשְׁתַּפֵּ֣ךְ נַפְשִׁ֑י יֹ֭אחֲז֣וּנִי יְמֵי־עֹֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And now my soul is poured out within me; Days of affliction have taken hold upon me.
					\item[NIV] “And now my life ebbs away; days of suffering grip me.
					\item[NRSV] “And now my soul is poured out within me; days of affliction have taken hold of me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 30:17} % *JOB-30-17
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-30-17}

			My bones are pierced in me in the night season: and my sinews take no rest.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לַ֗יְלָה עֲ֭צָמַי נִקַּ֣ר מֵעָלָ֑י וְ֝עֹרְקַ֗י לֹ֣א יִשְׁכָּבֽוּן׃}

					\item[NRSV] The night racks my bones, and the pain that gnaws me takes no rest.

	\subsubsection{Job 31} % *JOB-31
	% -----
		\label{JOB-31}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 31:1} % *JOB-31-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-31-1}

			I made a covenant with mine eyes; why then should I think upon a maid?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭רִית כָּרַ֣תִּי לְעֵינָ֑י וּמָ֥ה אֶ֝תְבּוֹנֵ֗ן עַל־בְּתוּלָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I made a covenant with mine eyes; How then should I look upon a virgin?
					\item[NIV] “I made a covenant with my eyes not to look lustfully at a young woman.
					\item[NRSV] “I have made a covenant with my eyes; how then could I look upon a virgin?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 31:2} % *JOB-31-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-31-2}

			For what portion of God is there from above? and what inheritance of the Almighty from on high?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמֶ֤ה ׀ חֵ֣לֶק אֱל֣וֹהַּ מִמָּ֑עַל וְֽנַחֲלַ֥ת שַׁ֝דַּ֗י מִמְּרֹמִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For what is the portion from God above, And the heritage from the Almighty on high?
					\item[NIV] For what is our lot from God above, our heritage from the Almighty on high?
					\item[NRSV] What would be my portion from God above, and my heritage from the Almighty on high?

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 31:3} % *JOB-31-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-31-3}

			Is not destruction to the wicked? and a strange punishment to the workers of iniquity?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲלֹא־אֵ֥יד לְעַוָּ֑ל וְ֝נֵ֗כֶר לְפֹ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Is it not ruin for the wicked, disaster for those who do wrong?
					\item[NRSV] Does not calamity befall the unrighteous, and disaster the workers of iniquity?

		\paragraph{Job 31:4} % *JOB-31-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-31-4}

			Doth not he see my ways, and count all my steps?

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 31:40} % *JOB-31-40
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-31-40}

			Let thistles grow instead of wheat, and cockle instead of barley. The words of Job are ended.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּ֤חַת חִטָּ֨ה ׀ יֵ֥צֵא ח֗וֹחַ וְתַֽחַת־שְׂעֹרָ֥ה בָאְשָׁ֑ה תַּ֝֗מּוּ דִּבְרֵ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thistles grow instead of wheat, And cockle instead of barley. The words of Job are ended.
					\item[NIV] then let briers come up instead of wheat and stinkweed instead of barley.” The words of Job are ended.
					\item[NRSV] let thorns grow instead of wheat, and foul weeds instead of barley.” The words of Job are ended.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Job 32} % *JOB-32
	% -----
		\label{JOB-32}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:1} % *JOB-32-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-1}

			So these three men ceased to answer Job, because he was righteous in his own eyes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיִּשְׁבְּת֡וּ שְׁלֹ֤שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָ֭אֵלֶּה מֵעֲנ֣וֹת אֶת־אִיּ֑וֹב כִּ֤י ה֖וּא צַדִּ֣יק בְּעֵינָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So these three men ceased to answer Job, because he was righteous in his own eyes.
					\item[NIV] So these three men stopped answering Job, because he was righteous in his own eyes.
					\item[NRSV] So these three men ceased to answer Job, because he was righteous in his own eyes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:2} % *JOB-32-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-2}

			Then was kindled the wrath of Elihu the son of Barachel the Buzite, of the kindred of Ram: against Job was his wrath kindled, because he justified himself rather than God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּ֤חַר אַ֨ף ׀ אֱלִיה֣וּא בֶן־בַּרַכְאֵ֣ל הַבּוּזִי֮ מִמִּשְׁפַּ֪חַ֫ת רָ֥ם בְּ֭אִיּוֹב חָרָ֣ה אַפּ֑וֹ עַֽל־צַדְּק֥וֹ נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ מֵאֱלֹהִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then was kindled the wrath of Elihu the son of Barachel the Buzite, of the family of Ram: against Job was his wrath kindled, because he justified himself rather than God.
					\item[NIV] But Elihu son of Barakel the Buzite, of the family of Ram, became very angry with Job for justifying himself rather than God.
					\item[NRSV] Then Elihu son of Barachel the Buzite, of the family of Ram, became angry. He was angry at Job because he justified himself rather than God;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:3} % *JOB-32-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-3}

			Also against his three friends was his wrath kindled, because they had found no answer, and yet had condemned Job.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבִשְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת רֵעָיו֮ חָרָ֪ה אַ֫פּ֥וֹ עַ֤ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־מָצְא֣וּ מַעֲנֶ֑ה וַ֝יַּרְשִׁ֗יעוּ אֶת־אִיּֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Also against his three friends was his wrath kindled, because they had found no answer, and yet had condemned Job.
					\item[NIV] He was also angry with the three friends, because they had found no way to refute Job, and yet had condemned him.
					\item[NRSV] he was angry also at Job’s three friends because they had found no answer, though they had declared Job to be in the wrong.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:4} % *JOB-32-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-4}

			Now Elihu had waited till Job had spoken, because they were elder than he.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וֶֽאֱלִיה֗וּ חִכָּ֣ה אֶת־אִ֭יּוֹב בִּדְבָרִ֑ים כִּ֤י זְֽקֵנִים־הֵ֖מָּה מִמֶּ֣נּוּ לְיָמִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now Elihu had waited to speak unto Job, because they were elder than he.
					\item[NIV] Now Elihu had waited before speaking to Job because they were older than he.
					\item[NRSV] Now Elihu had waited to speak to Job, because they were older than he.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:5} % *JOB-32-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-5}

			When Elihu saw that there was no answer in the mouth of these three men, then his wrath was kindled.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֤רְא אֱלִיה֗וּא כִּ֘י אֵ֤ין מַעֲנֶ֗ה בְּ֭פִי שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים וַיִּ֥חַר אַפּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And when Elihu saw that there was no answer in the mouth of these three men, his wrath was kindled.
					\item[NIV] But when he saw that the three men had nothing more to say, his anger was aroused.
					\item[NRSV] But when Elihu saw that there was no answer in the mouths of these three men, he became angry.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:6} % *JOB-32-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-6}

			And Elihu the son of Barachel the Buzite answered and said, I am young, and ye are very old; wherefore I was afraid, and durst not shew you mine opinion.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֤עַן ׀ אֱלִיה֖וּא בֶן־בַּֽרַכְאֵ֥ל הַבּוּזִ֗י וַיֹּ֫אמַ֥ר צָ֘עִ֤יר אֲנִ֣י לְ֭יָמִים וְאַתֶּ֣ם יְשִׁישִׁ֑ים עַל־כֵּ֖ן זָחַ֥לְתִּי וָֽאִירָ֓א ׀ מֵחַוֺּ֖ת דֵּעִ֣י אֶתְכֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Elihu the son of Barachel the Buzite answered and said, I am young, and ye are very old; Wherefore I held back, and durst not show you mine opinion.
					\item[NIV] So Elihu son of Barakel the Buzite said: “I am young in years, and you are old; that is why I was fearful, not daring to tell you what I know.
					\item[NRSV] Elihu son of Barachel the Buzite answered: “I am young in years, and you are aged; therefore I was timid and afraid to declare my opinion to you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:7} % *JOB-32-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-7}

			I said, Days should speak, and multitude of years should teach wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֭מַרְתִּי יָמִ֣ים יְדַבֵּ֑רוּ וְרֹ֥ב שָׁ֝נִ֗ים יֹדִ֥יעוּ חָכְמָֽה׃}
