					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I said, Days should speak, And multitude of years should teach wisdom.
					\item[NIV] I thought, ‘Age should speak; advanced years should teach wisdom.’
					\item[NRSV] I said, ‘Let days speak, and many years teach wisdom.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:8} % *JOB-32-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-8}

			But there is a spirit in man: and the inspiration of the Almighty giveth them understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֭כֵן רֽוּחַ־הִ֣יא בֶאֱנ֑וֹשׁ וְנִשְׁמַ֖ת שַׁדַּ֣י תְּבִינֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But there is a spirit in man, And the breath of the Almighty giveth them understanding.
					\item[NIV] But it is the spirit in a person, the breath of the Almighty, that gives them understanding.
					\item[NRSV] But truly it is the spirit in a mortal, the breath of the Almighty, that makes for understanding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:9} % *JOB-32-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-9}

			Great men are not always wise: neither do the aged understand judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־רַבִּ֥ים יֶחְכָּ֑מוּ וּ֝זְקֵנִ֗ים יָבִ֥ינוּ מִשְׁפָּֽט׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is not the great that are wise, Nor the aged that understand justice.
					\item[NIV] It is not only the old who are wise, not only the aged who understand what is right.
					\item[NRSV] It is not the old that are wise, nor the aged that understand what is right.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:10} % *JOB-32-10
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-10}

			Therefore I said, Hearken to me; I also will shew mine opinion.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לָכֵ֣ן אָ֭מַרְתִּי שִׁמְעָה־לִּ֑י אֲחַוֶּ֖ה דֵּעִ֣י אַף־אָֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Therefore I said, Hearken to me; I also will show mine opinion.
					\item[NIV] “Therefore I say: Listen to me; I too will tell you what I know.
					\item[NRSV] Therefore I say, ‘Listen to me; let me also declare my opinion.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:11} % *JOB-32-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-11}

			Behold, I waited for your words; I gave ear to your reasons, whilst ye searched out what to say.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֤ן הוֹחַ֨לְתִּי לְֽדִבְרֵיכֶ֗ם אָ֭זִין עַד־תְּב֥וּנֹֽתֵיכֶ֑ם עַֽד־תַּחְקְר֥וּן מִלִּֽין׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, I waited for your words, I listened for your reasonings, Whilst ye searched out what to say.
					\item[NIV] I waited while you spoke, I listened to your reasoning; while you were searching for words,
					\item[NRSV] “See, I waited for your words, I listened for your wise sayings, while you searched out what to say.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:12} % *JOB-32-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-12}

			Yea, I attended unto you, and, behold, there was none of you that convinced Job, or that answered his words:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעָֽדֵיכֶ֗ם אֶתְבּ֫וֹנָ֥ן וְהִנֵּ֤ה אֵ֣ין לְאִיּ֣וֹב מוֹכִ֑יחַ עוֹנֶ֖ה אֲמָרָ֣יו מִכֶּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, I attended unto you, And, behold, there was none that convinced Job, Or that answered his words, among you.
					\item[NIV] I gave you my full attention. But not one of you has proved Job wrong; none of you has answered his arguments.
					\item[NRSV] I gave you my attention, but there was in fact no one that confuted Job, no one among you that answered his words.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:13} % *JOB-32-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-13}

			Lest ye should say, We have found out wisdom: God thrusteth him down, not man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּן־תֹּ֣֭אמְרוּ מָצָ֣אנוּ חָכְמָ֑ה אֵ֖ל יִדְּפֶ֣נּוּ לֹא־אִֽישׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Beware lest ye say, We have found wisdom; God may vanquish him, not man:
					\item[NIV] Do not say, ‘We have found wisdom; let God, not a man, refute him.’
					\item[NRSV] Yet do not say, ‘We have found wisdom; God may vanquish him, not a human.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:14} % *JOB-32-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-14}

			Now he hath not directed his words against me: neither will I answer him with your speeches.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְלֹא־עָרַ֣ךְ אֵלַ֣י מִלִּ֑ין וּ֝בְאִמְרֵיכֶ֗ם לֹ֣א אֲשִׁיבֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For he hath not directed his words against me; Neither will I answer him with your speeches.
					\item[NIV] But Job has not marshaled his words against me, and I will not answer him with your arguments.
					\item[NRSV] He has not directed his words against me, and I will not answer him with your speeches.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:15} % *JOB-32-15
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-15}

			They were amazed, they answered no more: they left off speaking.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַ֭תּוּ לֹא־עָ֣נוּ ע֑וֹד הֶעְתִּ֖יקוּ מֵהֶ֣ם מִלִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They are amazed, they answer no more: They have not a word to say.
					\item[NIV] “They are dismayed and have no more to say; words have failed them.
					\item[NRSV] “They are dismayed, they answer no more; they have not a word to say.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 32:16} % *JOB-32-16
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-32-16}

			When I had waited, (for they spake not, but stood still, and answered no more;)

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭הוֹחַלְתִּי כִּי־לֹ֣א יְדַבֵּ֑רוּ כִּ֥י עָ֝מְד֗וּ לֹא־עָ֥נוּ עֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]

			I said, I will answer also my part, I also will shew mine opinion.

			Let me not, I pray you, accept any man’s person, neither let me give flattering titles unto man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־נָ֭א אֶשָּׂ֣א פְנֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְאֶל־אָ֝דָ֗ם לֹ֣א אֲכַנֶּֽה׃}

					\item[NIV] I will show no partiality, nor will I flatter anyone;

			For I know not to give flattering titles; in so doing my maker would soon take me away.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י לֹ֣א יָדַ֣עְתִּי אֲכַנֶּ֑ה כִּ֝מְעַ֗ט יִשָּׂאֵ֥נִי עֹשֵֽׂנִי׃}

		\label{JOB-33}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 33:1} % *JOB-33-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-33-1}

			Wherefore, Job, I pray thee, hear my speeches, and hearken to all my words.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽאוּלָ֗ם שְׁמַֽע־נָ֣א אִיּ֣וֹב מִלָּ֑י וְֽכָל־דְּבָרַ֥י הַאֲזִֽינָה׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] “But now, Job, listen to my words; pay attention to everything I say.
					\item[NRSV] “But now, hear my speech, O Job, and listen to all my words.

			Behold, now I have opened my mouth, my tongue hath spoken in my mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הִנֵּה־נָ֭א פָּתַ֣חְתִּי פִ֑י דִּבְּרָ֖ה לְשׁוֹנִ֣י בְחִכִּֽי׃}

					\item[NRSV] See, I open my mouth; the tongue in my mouth speaks.

			My words shall be of the uprightness of my heart: and my lips shall utter knowledge clearly.

			\begin{versebox}

			Behold, in this thou art not just: I will answer thee, that God is greater than man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֶן־זֹ֣את לֹא־צָדַ֣קְתָּ אֶעֱנֶ֑ךָּ כִּֽי־יִרְבֶּ֥ה אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַ מֵאֱנֽוֹשׁ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] “But I tell you, in this you are not right, for God is greater than any mortal.

					\item[NIV] If you have anything to say, answer me; speak up, for I want to vindicate you.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-16}

			If now thou hast understanding, hear this: hearken to the voice of my words.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִם־בִּ֥ינָה שִׁמְעָה־זֹּ֑את הַ֝אֲזִ֗ינָה לְק֣וֹל מִלָּֽי׃}

					\item[NIV] “If you have understanding, hear this; listen to what I say.
					\item[NRSV] “If you have understanding, hear this; listen to what I say.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-17}

			Shall even he that hateth right govern? and wilt thou condemn him that is most just?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַאַ֬ף שׂוֹנֵ֣א מִשְׁפָּ֣ט יַחֲב֑וֹשׁ וְאִם־צַדִּ֖יק כַּבִּ֣יר תַּרְשִֽׁיעַ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Can someone who hates justice govern? Will you condemn the just and mighty One?
					\item[NRSV] Shall one who hates justice govern? Will you condemn one who is righteous and mighty,

		\paragraph{Job 34:18} % *JOB-34-18
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-18}

			Is it fit to say to a king, Thou art wicked? and to princes, Ye are ungodly?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַאֲמֹ֣ר לְמֶ֣לֶךְ בְּלִיָּ֑עַל רָ֝שָׁ֗ע אֶל־נְדִיבִֽים׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Is he not the One who says to kings, ‘You are worthless,’ and to nobles, ‘You are wicked,’
					\item[NRSV] who says to a king, ‘You scoundrel!’ and to princes, ‘You wicked men!’;

		\paragraph{Job 34:19} % *JOB-34-19
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-19}

			How much less to him that accepteth not the persons of princes, nor regardeth the rich more than the poor? for they all are the work of his hands.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־נָשָׂ֨א ׀ פְּנֵ֥י שָׂרִ֗ים וְלֹ֣א נִכַּר־שׁ֭וֹעַ לִפְנֵי־דָ֑ל כִּֽי־מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה יָדָ֣יו כֻּלָּֽם׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[ASV] That respecteth not the persons of princes, Nor regardeth the rich more than the poor; For they all are the work of his hands.
					\item[NIV] who shows no partiality to princes and does not favor the rich over the poor, for they are all the work of his hands?
					\item[NRSV] who shows no partiality to nobles, nor regards the rich more than the poor, for they are all the work of his hands?

		\paragraph{Job 34:20} % *JOB-34-20
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-20}

			In a moment shall they die, and the people shall be troubled at midnight, and pass away: and the mighty shall be taken away without hand.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רֶ֤גַע ׀ יָמֻתוּ֮ וַחֲצ֪וֹת לָ֥יְלָה יְגֹעֲשׁ֣וּ עָ֣ם וְיַעֲבֹ֑רוּ וְיָסִ֥ירוּ אַ֝בִּ֗יר לֹ֣א בְיָֽד׃}

					\item[ASV] In a moment they die, even at midnight; The people are shaken and pass away, And the mighty are taken away without hand.
					\item[NIV] They die in an instant, in the middle of the night; the people are shaken and they pass away; the mighty are removed without human hand.
					\item[NRSV] In a moment they die; at midnight the people are shaken and pass away, and the mighty are taken away by no human hand.

			For he will not lay upon man more than right; that he should enter into judgment with God.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-28}

			So that they cause the cry of the poor to come unto him, and he heareth the cry of the afflicted.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְהָבִ֣יא עָ֭לָיו צַֽעֲקַת־דָּ֑ל וְצַעֲקַ֖ת עֲנִיִּ֣ים יִשְׁמָֽע׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] They caused the cry of the poor to come before him, so that he heard the cry of the needy.
					\item[NRSV] so that they caused the cry of the poor to come to him, and he heard the cry of the afflicted—

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 34:29} % *JOB-34-29
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-29}

			When he giveth quietness, who then can make trouble? and when he hideth his face, who then can behold him? whether it be done against a nation, or against a man only:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְה֤וּא יַשְׁקִ֨ט ׀ וּמִ֥י יַרְשִׁ֗עַ וְיַסְתֵּ֣ר פָּ֭נִים וּמִ֣י יְשׁוּרֶ֑נּוּ וְעַל־גּ֖וֹי וְעַל־אָדָ֣ם יָֽחַד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When he giveth quietness, who then can condemn? And when he hideth his face, who then can behold him? Alike whether it be done unto a nation, or unto a man:
					\item[NIV] But if he remains silent, who can condemn him? If he hides his face, who can see him? Yet he is over individual and nation alike,
					\item[NRSV] When he is quiet, who can condemn? When he hides his face, who can behold him, whether it be a nation or an individual?—
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-30}

			That the hypocrite reign not, lest the people be ensnared.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭מְּלֹךְ אָדָ֥ם חָנֵ֗ף מִמֹּ֥קְשֵׁי עָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That the godless man reign not, That there be none to ensnare the people.
					\item[NIV] to keep the godless from ruling, from laying snares for the people.
					\item[NRSV] so that the godless should not reign, or those who ensnare the people.
					% % \item[OTX*]

		\paragraph{Job 34:31} % *JOB-34-31
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-31}

			Surely it is meet to be said unto God, I have borne chastisement, I will not offend any more:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־אֶל־אֵ֭ל הֶאָמַ֥ר נָשָׂ֗אתִי לֹ֣א אֶחְבֹּֽל׃}

					\item[NIV] “Suppose someone says to God, ‘I am guilty but will offend no more.
					\item[NRSV] “For has anyone said to God, ‘I have endured punishment; I will not offend any more;

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-32}

			That which I see not teach thou me: if I have done iniquity, I will do no more.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּלְעֲדֵ֣י אֶ֭חֱזֶה אַתָּ֣ה הֹרֵ֑נִי אִֽם־עָ֥וֶל פָּ֝עַ֗לְתִּי לֹ֣א אֹסִֽיף׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That which I see not teach thou me: If I have done iniquity, I will do it no more?
					\item[NIV] Teach me what I cannot see; if I have done wrong, I will not do so again.’
					\item[NRSV] teach me what I do not see; if I have done iniquity, I will do it no more’?

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-34-33}

			Should it be according to thy mind? he will recompense it, whether thou refuse, or whether thou choose; and not I: therefore speak what thou knowest.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַֽמֵעִמְּךָ֬ יְשַׁלְמֶ֨נָּה כִּֽי־מָאַ֗סְתָּ כִּי־אַתָּ֣ה תִבְחַ֣ר וְלֹא־אָ֑נִי וּֽמַה־יָדַ֥עְתָּ דַבֵּֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NRSV] Will he then pay back to suit you, because you reject it? For you must choose, and not I; therefore declare what you know.

					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How much less when thou sayest thou beholdest him not, The cause is before him, and thou waitest for him!
					\item[NIV] How much less, then, will he listen when you say that you do not see him, that your case is before him and you must wait for him,
					\item[NRSV] How much less when you say that you do not see him, that the case is before him, and you are waiting for him!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 35:15} % *JOB-35-15
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-35-15}

			But now, because it is not so, he hath visited in his anger; yet he knoweth it not in great extremity:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֗ה כִּי־אַ֭יִן פָּקַ֣ד אַפּ֑וֹ וְלֹֽא־יָדַ֖ע בַּפַּ֣שׁ מְאֹֽד׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But now, because he hath not visited in his anger, Neither doth he greatly regard arrogance;
					\item[NIV] and further, that his anger never punishes and he does not take the least notice of wickedness.
					\item[NRSV] And now, because his anger does not punish, and he does not greatly heed transgression,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 35:16} % *JOB-35-16
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-35-16}

			Therefore doth Job open his mouth in vain; he multiplieth words without knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭אִיּוֹב הֶ֣בֶל יִפְצֶה־פִּ֑יהוּ בִּבְלִי־דַ֝֗עַת מִלִּ֥ין יַכְבִּֽר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Therefore doth Job open his mouth in vanity; He multiplieth words without knowledge.
					\item[NIV] So Job opens his mouth with empty talk; without knowledge he multiplies words.”
					\item[NRSV] Job opens his mouth in empty talk, he multiplies words without knowledge.”

			\label{JOB-36-2}

			Suffer me a little, and I will shew thee that I have yet to speak on God’s behalf.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּתַּר־לִ֣י זְ֭עֵיר וַאֲחַוֶּ֑ךָּ כִּ֤י ע֖וֹד לֶאֱל֣וֹהַּ מִלִּֽים׃}

					\item[NIV] “Bear with me a little longer and I will show you that there is more to be said in God’s behalf.
					\item[NRSV] “Bear with me a little, and I will show you, for I have yet something to say on God’s behalf.

			\label{JOB-36-3}

			I will fetch my knowledge from afar, and will ascribe righteousness to my Maker.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶשָּׂ֣א דֵ֭עִי לְמֵרָח֑וֹק וּ֝לְפֹעֲלִ֗י אֶֽתֵּֽן־צֶֽדֶק׃}

					\item[NIV] I get my knowledge from afar; I will ascribe justice to my Maker.
					\item[NRSV] I will bring my knowledge from far away, and ascribe righteousness to my Maker.

			\label{JOB-36-4}

			For truly my words shall not be false: he that is perfect in knowledge is with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־אָ֭מְנָם לֹא־שֶׁ֣קֶר מִלָּ֑י תְּמִ֖ים דֵּע֣וֹת עִמָּֽךְ׃}

					\item[NIV] Be assured that my words are not false; one who has perfect knowledge is with you.
					\item[NRSV] For truly my words are not false; one who is perfect in knowledge is with you.

			Behold, God is mighty, and despiseth not any: he is mighty in strength and wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֶן־אֵ֣ל כַּ֭בִּיר וְלֹ֣א יִמְאָ֑ס כַּ֝בִּ֗יר כֹּ֣חַֽ לֵֽב׃}

					\item[NIV] “God is mighty, but despises no one; he is mighty, and firm in his purpose.
					\item[NRSV] “Surely God is mighty and does not despise any; he is mighty in strength of understanding.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-6}

			He preserveth not the life of the wicked: but giveth right to the poor.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יְחַיֶּ֥ה רָשָׁ֑ע וּמִשְׁפַּ֖ט עֲנִיִּ֣ים יִתֵּֽן׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He preserveth not the life of the wicked, But giveth to the afflicted their right.
					\item[NIV] He does not keep the wicked alive but gives the afflicted their rights.
					\item[NRSV] He does not keep the wicked alive, but gives the afflicted their right.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 36:7} % *JOB-36-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-7}

			He withdraweth not his eyes from the righteous: but with kings are they on the throne; yea, he doth establish them for ever, and they are exalted.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־יִגְרַ֥ע מִצַּדִּ֗יק עֵ֫ינָ֥יו וְאֶת־מְלָכִ֥ים לַכִּסֵּ֑א וַיֹּשִׁיבֵ֥ם לָ֝נֶ֗צַח וַיִּגְבָּֽהוּ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He withdraweth not his eyes from the righteous: But with kings upon the throne He setteth them for ever, and they are exalted.
					\item[NIV] He does not take his eyes off the righteous; he enthrones them with kings and exalts them forever.
					\item[NRSV] He does not withdraw his eyes from the righteous, but with kings on the throne he sets them forever, and they are exalted.

			\label{JOB-36-8}

			And if they be bound in fetters, and be holden in cords of affliction;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִם־אֲסוּרִ֥ים בַּזִּקִּ֑ים יִ֝לָּכְד֗וּן בְּחַבְלֵי־עֹֽנִי׃}

					\item[NRSV] And if they are bound in fetters and caught in the cords of affliction,

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-9}

			Then he sheweth them their work, and their transgressions that they have exceeded.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּגֵּ֣ד לָהֶ֣ם פָּעֳלָ֑ם וּ֝פִשְׁעֵיהֶ֗ם כִּ֣י יִתְגַּבָּֽרוּ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then he showeth them their work, And their transgressions, that they have behaved themselves proudly.
					\item[NIV] he tells them what they have done— that they have sinned arrogantly.
					\item[NRSV] then he declares to them their work and their transgressions, that they are behaving arrogantly.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-10}

			He openeth also their ear to discipline, and commandeth that they return from iniquity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּ֣גֶל אָ֭זְנָם לַמּוּסָ֑ר וַ֝יֹּ֗אמֶר כִּֽי־יְשֻׁב֥וּן מֵאָֽוֶן׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] He makes them listen to correction and commands them to repent of their evil.
					\item[NRSV] He opens their ears to instruction, and commands that they return from iniquity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 36:11} % *JOB-36-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-11}

			If they obey and serve him, they shall spend their days in prosperity, and their years in pleasures.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִֽם־יִשְׁמְע֗וּ וְֽיַ֫עֲבֹ֥דוּ יְכַלּ֣וּ יְמֵיהֶ֣ם בַּטּ֑וֹב וּ֝שְׁנֵיהֶ֗ם בַּנְּעִימִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If they hearken and serve him, They shall spend their days in prosperity, And their years in pleasures.
					\item[NIV] If they obey and serve him, they will spend the rest of their days in prosperity and their years in contentment.
					\item[NRSV] If they listen, and serve him, they complete their days in prosperity, and their years in pleasantness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 36:12} % *JOB-36-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-12}

			But if they obey not, they shall perish by the sword, and they shall die without knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִם־לֹ֣א יִ֭שְׁמְעוּ בְּשֶׁ֣לַח יַעֲבֹ֑רוּ וְ֝יִגְוְע֗וּ כִּבְלִי־דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But if they hearken not, they shall perish by the sword, And they shall die without knowledge.
					\item[NIV] But if they do not listen, they will perish by the sword and die without knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] But if they do not listen, they shall perish by the sword, and die without knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 36:13} % *JOB-36-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-13}

			But the hypocrites in heart heap up wrath: they cry not when he bindeth them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽחַנְפֵי־לֵ֭ב יָשִׂ֣ימוּ אָ֑ף לֹ֥א יְ֝שַׁוְּע֗וּ כִּ֣י אֲסָרָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But they that are godless in heart lay up anger: They cry not for help when he bindeth them.
					\item[NIV] “The godless in heart harbor resentment; even when he fetters them, they do not cry for help.
					\item[NRSV] “The godless in heart cherish anger; they do not cry for help when he binds them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 36:14} % *JOB-36-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-14}

			They die in youth, and their life is among the unclean.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תָּמֹ֣ת בַּנֹּ֣עַר נַפְשָׁ֑ם וְ֝חַיָּתָ֗ם בַּקְּדֵשִֽׁים׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They die in youth, And their life perisheth among the unclean.
					\item[NIV] They die in their youth, among male prostitutes of the shrines.
					\item[NRSV] They die in their youth, and their life ends in shame.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 36:15} % *JOB-36-15
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-15}

			He delivereth the poor in his affliction, and openeth their ears in oppression.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְחַלֵּ֣ץ עָנִ֣י בְעָנְי֑וֹ וְיִ֖גֶל בַּלַּ֣חַץ אָזְנָֽם׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He delivereth the afflicted by their affliction, And openeth their ear in oppression.
					\item[NIV] But those who suffer he delivers in their suffering; he speaks to them in their affliction.
					\item[NRSV] He delivers the afflicted by their affliction, and opens their ear by adversity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 36:16} % *JOB-36-16
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-36-16}

			Even so would he have removed thee out of the strait into a broad place, where there is no straitness; and that which should be set on thy table should be full of fatness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאַ֤ף הֲסִיתְךָ֨ ׀ מִפִּי־צָ֗ר רַ֭חַב לֹא־מוּצָ֣ק תַּחְתֶּ֑יהָ וְנַ֥חַת שֻׁ֝לְחָנְךָ֗ מָ֣לֵא דָֽשֶׁן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] “He is wooing you from the jaws of distress to a spacious place free from restriction, to the comfort of your table laden with choice food.
					\item[NRSV] He also allured you out of distress into a broad place where there was no constraint, and what was set on your table was full of fatness.

					\item[NRSV] Beware that wrath does not entice you into scoffing, and do not let the greatness of the ransom turn you aside.

					\item[NIV] He fills his hands with lightning and commands it to strike its mark.

	\subsubsection{Job 37} % *JOB-37
	% -----
		\label{JOB-37}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 37:1} % *JOB-37-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-1}

			At this also my heart trembleth, and is moved out of his place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַף־לְ֭זֹאת יֶחֱרַ֣ד לִבִּ֑י וְ֝יִתַּ֗ר מִמְּקוֹמֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, at this my heart trembleth, And is moved out of its place.
					\item[NIV] “At this my heart pounds and leaps from its place.
					\item[NRSV] “At this also my heart trembles, and leaps out of its place.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 37:2} % *JOB-37-2
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-2}

			Hear attentively the noise of his voice, and the sound that goeth out of his mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׁמְע֤וּ שָׁמ֣וֹעַ בְּרֹ֣גֶז קֹל֑וֹ וְ֝הֶ֗גֶה מִפִּ֥יו יֵצֵֽא׃}

					\item[NIV] Listen! Listen to the roar of his voice, to the rumbling that comes from his mouth.
					\item[NRSV] Listen, listen to the thunder of his voice and the rumbling that comes from his mouth.

			He directeth it under the whole heaven, and his lightning unto the ends of the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַחֲרָ֤יו ׀ יִשְׁאַג־ק֗וֹל יַ֭רְעֵם בְּק֣וֹל גְּאוֹנ֑וֹ וְלֹ֥א יְ֝עַקְּבֵ֗ם כִּֽי־יִשָּׁמַ֥ע קוֹלֽוֹ׃}

					\item[NRSV] After it his voice roars; he thunders with his majestic voice and he does not restrain the lightnings when his voice is heard.

		\paragraph{Job 37:20} % *JOB-37-20
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-20}

			Shall it be told him that I speak? if a man speak, surely he shall be swallowed up.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַֽיְסֻפַּר־ל֭וֹ כִּ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר אִֽם־אָ֥מַר אִ֝֗ישׁ כִּ֣י יְבֻלָּֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Shall it be told him that I would speak? Or should a man wish that he were swallowed up?
					\item[NIV] Should he be told that I want to speak? Would anyone ask to be swallowed up?
					\item[NRSV] Should he be told that I want to speak? Did anyone ever wish to be swallowed up?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 37:21} % *JOB-37-21
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-21}

			And now men see not the bright light which is in the clouds: but the wind passeth, and cleanseth them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֤ה ׀ לֹ֘א רָ֤אוּ א֗וֹר בָּהִ֣יר ה֭וּא בַּשְּׁחָקִ֑ים וְר֥וּחַ עָ֝בְרָ֗ה וַֽתְּטַהֲרֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And now men see not the light which is bright in the skies; But the wind passeth, and cleareth them.
					\item[NIV] Now no one can look at the sun, bright as it is in the skies after the wind has swept them clean.
					\item[NRSV] Now, no one can look on the light when it is bright in the skies, when the wind has passed and cleared them.

		\paragraph{Job 37:22} % *JOB-37-22
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-22}

			Fair weather cometh out of the north: with God is terrible majesty.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭צָּפוֹן זָהָ֣ב יֶֽאֱתֶ֑ה עַל־אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ נ֣וֹרָא הֽוֹד׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Out of the north he comes in golden splendor; God comes in awesome majesty.
					\item[NRSV] Out of the north comes golden splendor; around God is awesome majesty.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-23}

			Touching the Almighty, we cannot find him out: he is excellent in power, and in judgment, and in plenty of justice: he will not afflict.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שַׁדַּ֣י לֹֽא־מְ֭צָאנֻהוּ שַׂגִּיא־כֹ֑חַ וּמִשְׁפָּ֥ט וְרֹב־צְ֝דָקָ֗ה לֹ֣א יְעַנֶּֽה׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Touching the Almighty, we cannot find him out: He is excellent in power; And in justice and plenteous righteousness he will not afflict.
					\item[NIV] The Almighty is beyond our reach and exalted in power; in his justice and great righteousness, he does not oppress.
					\item[NRSV] The Almighty —we cannot find him; he is great in power and justice, and abundant righteousness he will not violate.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-37-24}

			Men do therefore fear him: he respecteth not any that are wise of heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לָ֭כֵן יְרֵא֣וּהוּ אֲנָשִׁ֑ים לֹֽא־יִ֝רְאֶ֗ה כָּל־חַכְמֵי־לֵֽב׃}

					\item[ASV] Men do therefore fear him: He regardeth not any that are wise of heart.
					\item[NIV] Therefore, people revere him, for does he not have regard for all the wise in heart?”
					\item[NRSV] Therefore mortals fear him; he does not regard any who are wise in their own conceit.”

			\label{JOB-38-32}

			Doth the eagle mount up at thy command, and make her nest on high?

	\subsubsection{Job 41} % *JOB-41
	% -----
		\label{JOB-41}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 41:1} % *JOB-41-1
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-1}

			Canst thou draw out leviathan with an hook? or his tongue with a cord which thou lettest down?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תִּמְשֹׁ֣ךְ לִוְיָתָ֣ן בְּחַכָּ֑ה וּ֝בְחֶ֗בֶל תַּשְׁקִ֥יעַ לְשֹׁנֽוֹ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] “Can you pull in Leviathan with a fishhook or tie down its tongue with a rope?
					\item[NRSV] “Can you draw out Leviathan with a fishhook, or press down its tongue with a cord?

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-2}

			Canst thou put an hook into his nose? or bore his jaw through with a thorn?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲתָשִׂ֣ים אַגְמ֣וֹן בְּאַפּ֑וֹ וּ֝בְח֗וֹחַ תִּקּ֥וֹב לֶֽחֱיוֹ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Can you put a cord through its nose or pierce its jaw with a hook?
					\item[NRSV] Can you put a rope in its nose, or pierce its jaw with a hook?

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-3}

			Will he make many supplications unto thee? will he speak soft words unto thee?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲיַרְבֶּ֣ה אֵ֭לֶיךָ תַּחֲנוּנִ֑ים אִם־יְדַבֵּ֖ר אֵלֶ֣יךָ רַכּֽוֹת׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Will it keep begging you for mercy? Will it speak to you with gentle words?
					\item[NRSV] Will it make many supplications to you? Will it speak soft words to you?

		\paragraph{Job 41:4} % *JOB-41-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-4}

			Will he make a covenant with thee? wilt thou take him for a servant for ever?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲיִכְרֹ֣ת בְּרִ֣ית עִמָּ֑ךְ תִּ֝קָּחֶ֗נּוּ לְעֶ֣בֶד עוֹלָֽם׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Will he make a covenant with thee, That thou shouldest take him for a servant for ever?
					\item[NIV] Will it make an agreement with you for you to take it as your slave for life?
					\item[NRSV] Will it make a covenant with you to be taken as your servant forever?

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Job 41:5} % *JOB-41-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-5}

			Wilt thou play with him as with a bird? or wilt thou bind him for thy maidens?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַֽתְשַׂחֶק־בּ֭וֹ כַּצִּפּ֑וֹר וְ֝תִקְשְׁרֶ֗נּוּ לְנַעֲרוֹתֶֽיךָ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Can you make a pet of it like a bird or put it on a leash for the young women in your house?
					\item[NRSV] Will you play with it as with a bird, or will you put it on leash for your girls?

		\paragraph{Job 41:6} % *JOB-41-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-6}

			Shall the companions make a banquet of him? shall they part him among the merchants?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִכְר֣וּ עָ֭לָיו חַבָּרִ֑ים יֶ֝חֱצ֗וּהוּ בֵּ֣ין כְּֽנַעֲנִֽים׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Will the bands of fishermen make traffic of him? Will they part him among the merchants?
					\item[NIV] Will traders barter for it? Will they divide it up among the merchants?
					\item[NRSV] Will traders bargain over it? Will they divide it up among the merchants?

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-7}

			Canst thou fill his skin with barbed irons? or his head with fish spears?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַֽתְמַלֵּ֣א בְשֻׂכּ֣וֹת עוֹר֑וֹ וּבְצִלְצַ֖ל דָּגִ֣ים רֹאשֽׁוֹ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Can you fill its hide with harpoons or its head with fishing spears?
					\item[NRSV] Can you fill its skin with harpoons, or its head with fishing spears?

		\paragraph{Job 41:8} % *JOB-41-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-8}

			Lay thine hand upon him, remember the battle, do no more.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂים־עָלָ֥יו כַּפֶּ֑ךָ זְכֹ֥ר מִ֝לְחָמָ֗ה אַל־תּוֹסַֽף׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lay thy hand upon him; Remember the battle, and do so no more.
					\item[NIV] If you lay a hand on it, you will remember the struggle and never do it again!
					\item[NRSV] Lay hands on it; think of the battle; you will not do it again!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 41:9} % *JOB-41-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-9}

			Behold, the hope of him is in vain: shall not one be cast down even at the sight of him?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵן־תֹּחַלְתּ֥וֹ נִכְזָ֑בָה הֲגַ֖ם אֶל־מַרְאָ֣יו יֻטָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, the hope of him is in vain: Will not one be cast down even at the sight of him?
					\item[NIV] Any hope of subduing it is false; the mere sight of it is overpowering.
					\item[NRSV] Any hope of capturing it will be disappointed; were not even the gods overwhelmed at the sight of it?

			\begin{versebox}

					\item[NRSV] The folds of its flesh cling together; it is firmly cast and immovable.

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-41-25}

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭שֵּׂתוֹ יָג֣וּרוּ אֵלִ֑ים מִ֝שְּׁבָרִ֗ים יִתְחַטָּֽאוּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] When it rises up, the mighty are terrified; they retreat before its thrashing.
					\item[NRSV] When it raises itself up the gods are afraid; at the crashing they are beside themselves.

			The arrow cannot make him flee: slingstones are turned with him into stubble.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־יַבְרִיחֶ֥נּוּ בֶן־קָ֑שֶׁת לְ֝קַ֗שׁ נֶהְפְּכוּ־ל֥וֹ אַבְנֵי־קָֽלַע׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]

					\item[ASV] The arrow cannot make him flee: Sling-stones are turned with him into stubble.
					\item[NIV] Arrows do not make it flee; slingstones are like chaff to it.
					\item[NRSV] The arrow cannot make it flee; slingstones, for it, are turned to chaff.

			Darts are counted as stubble: he laugheth at the shaking of a spear.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּ֭קַשׁ נֶחְשְׁב֣וּ תוֹתָ֑ח וְ֝יִשְׂחַ֗ק לְרַ֣עַשׁ כִּידֽוֹן׃}

					\item[NRSV] Clubs are counted as chaff; it laughs at the rattle of javelins.

					\item[NRSV] Its underparts are like sharp potsherds; it spreads itself like a threshing sledge on the mire.

			He maketh a path to shine after him; one would think the deep to be hoary.

					\item[BHS] \he{אַ֭חֲרָיו יָאִ֣יר נָתִ֑יב יַחְשֹׁ֖ב תְּה֣וֹם לְשֵׂיבָֽה׃}

					\item[BHS] \he{אֵֽין־עַל־עָפָ֥ר מָשְׁל֑וֹ הֶ֝עָשׂ֗וּ לִבְלִי־חָֽת׃}

		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-2}

			I know that thou canst do every thing, and that no thought can be withholden from thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָ֭דַעְתִּי כִּי־כֹ֣ל תּוּכָ֑ל וְלֹא־יִבָּצֵ֖ר מִמְּךָ֣ מְזִמָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I know that thou canst do all things, And that no purpose of thine can be restrained.
					\item[NIV] “I know that you can do all things; no purpose of yours can be thwarted.
					\item[NRSV] “I know that you can do all things, and that no purpose of yours can be thwarted.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:3} % *JOB-42-3
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-3}

			Who is he that hideth counsel without knowledge? therefore have I uttered that I understood not; things too wonderful for me, which I knew not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֤י זֶ֨ה ׀ מַעְלִ֥ים עֵצָ֗ה בְּֽלִ֫י דָ֥עַת לָכֵ֣ן הִ֭גַּדְתִּי וְלֹ֣א אָבִ֑ין נִפְלָא֥וֹת מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי וְלֹ֣א אֵדָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who is this that hideth counsel without knowledge? Therefore have I uttered that which I understood not, Things too wonderful for me, which I knew not.
					\item[NIV] You asked, ‘Who is this that obscures my plans without knowledge?’ Surely I spoke of things I did not understand, things too wonderful for me to know.
					\item[NRSV] ‘Who is this that hides counsel without knowledge?’ Therefore I have uttered what I did not understand, things too wonderful for me, which I did not know.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		\paragraph{Job 42:4} % *JOB-42-4
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-4}

			Hear, I beseech thee, and I will speak: I will demand of thee, and declare thou unto me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְֽׁמַֽע־נָ֭א וְאָנֹכִ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר אֶ֝שְׁאָלְךָ֗ וְהוֹדִיעֵֽנִי׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] “You said, ‘Listen now, and I will speak; I will question you, and you shall answer me.’
					\item[NRSV] ‘Hear, and I will speak; I will question you, and you declare to me.’

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:5} % *JOB-42-5
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-5}

			I have heard of thee by the hearing of the ear: but now mine eye seeth thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְשֵֽׁמַע־אֹ֥זֶן שְׁמַעְתִּ֑יךָ וְ֝עַתָּ֗ה עֵינִ֥י רָאָֽתְךָ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I had heard of thee by the hearing of the ear; But now mine eye seeth thee:
					\item[NIV] My ears had heard of you but now my eyes have seen you.
					\item[NRSV] I had heard of you by the hearing of the ear, but now my eye sees you;

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:6} % *JOB-42-6
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-6}

			Wherefore I abhor myself, and repent in dust and ashes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַל־כֵּ֭ן אֶמְאַ֣ס וְנִחַ֑מְתִּי עַל־עָפָ֥ר וָאֵֽפֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wherefore I abhor myself, And repent in dust and ashes.
					\item[NIV] Therefore I despise myself and repent in dust and ashes.”
					\item[NRSV] therefore I despise myself, and repent in dust and ashes.”

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:7} % *JOB-42-7
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-7}

			And it was so, that after the LORD had spoken these words unto Job, the LORD said to Eliphaz the Temanite, My wrath is kindled against thee, and against thy two friends: for ye have not spoken of me the thing that is right, as my servant Job hath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֗י אַחַ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהוָ֛ה אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה אֶל־אִיּ֑וֹב וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהוָ֜ה אֶל־אֱלִיפַ֣ז הַתֵּֽימָנִ֗י חָרָ֨ה אַפִּ֤י בְךָ֙ וּבִשְׁנֵ֣י רֵעֶ֔יךָ כִּ֠י לֹ֣א דִבַּרְתֶּ֥ם אֵלַ֛י נְכוֹנָ֖ה כְּעַבְדִּ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it was so, that, after Jehovah had spoken these words unto Job, Jehovah said to Eliphaz the Temanite, My wrath is kindled against thee, and against thy two friends; for ye have not spoken of me the thing that is right, as my servant Job hath.
					\item[NIV] After the LORD had said these things to Job, he said to Eliphaz the Temanite, “I am angry with you and your two friends, because you have not spoken the truth about me, as my servant Job has.
					\item[NRSV] After the LORD had spoken these words to Job, the LORD said to Eliphaz the Temanite: “My wrath is kindled against you and against your two friends; for you have not spoken of me what is right, as my servant Job has.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:8} % *JOB-42-8
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-8}

			Therefore take unto you now seven bullocks and seven rams, and go to my servant Job, and offer up for yourselves a burnt offering; and my servant Job shall pray for you: for him will I accept: lest I deal with you after your folly, in that ye have not spoken of me the thing which is right, like my servant Job.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֡ה קְחֽוּ־לָכֶ֣ם שִׁבְעָֽה־פָרִים֩ וְשִׁבְעָ֨ה אֵילִ֜ים וּלְכ֣וּ ׀ אֶל־עַבְדִּ֣י אִיּ֗וֹב וְהַעֲלִיתֶ֤ם עוֹלָה֙ בַּֽעַדְכֶ֔ם וְאִיּ֣וֹב עַבְדִּ֔י יִתְפַּלֵּ֖ל עֲלֵיכֶ֑ם כִּ֧י אִם־פָּנָ֣יו אֶשָּׂ֗א לְבִלְתִּ֞י עֲשׂ֤וֹת עִמָּכֶם֙ נְבָלָ֔ה כִּ֠י לֹ֣א דִבַּרְתֶּ֥ם אֵלַ֛י נְכוֹנָ֖ה כְּעַבְדִּ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now therefore, take unto you seven bullocks and seven rams, and go to my servant Job, and offer up for yourselves a burnt-offering; and my servant Job shall pray for you; for him will I accept, that I deal not with you after your folly; for ye have not spoken of me the thing that is right, as my servant Job hath.
					\item[NIV] So now take seven bulls and seven rams and go to my servant Job and sacrifice a burnt offering for yourselves. My servant Job will pray for you, and I will accept his prayer and not deal with you according to your folly. You have not spoken the truth about me, as my servant Job has.”
					\item[NRSV] Now therefore take seven bulls and seven rams, and go to my servant Job, and offer up for yourselves a burnt offering; and my servant Job shall pray for you, for I will accept his prayer not to deal with you according to your folly; for you have not spoken of me what is right, as my servant Job has done.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:9} % *JOB-42-9
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-9}

			So Eliphaz the Temanite and Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite went, and did according as the LORD commanded them: the LORD also accepted Job.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּלְכוּ֩ אֱלִיפַ֨ז הַתֵּֽימָנִ֜י וּבִלְדַּ֣ד הַשּׁוּחִ֗י צֹפַר֙ הַנַּ֣עֲמָתִ֔י וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֔וּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר דִּבֶּ֥ר אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם יְהוָ֑ה וַיִּשָּׂ֥א יְהוָ֖ה אֶת־פְּנֵ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Eliphaz the Temanite and Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite went, and did according as Jehovah commanded them: and Jehovah accepted Job.
					\item[NIV] So Eliphaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite did what the LORD told them; and the LORD accepted Job’s prayer.
					\item[NRSV] So Eliphaz the Temanite and Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite went and did what the LORD had told them; and the LORD accepted Job’s prayer.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:10} % *JOB-42-10
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-10}

			And the LORD turned the captivity of Job, when he prayed for his friends: also the LORD gave Job twice as much as he had before.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיהוָ֗ה שָׁ֚ב שְׁב֣וּת אִיּ֔וֹב בְּהִֽתְפַּֽלְל֖וֹ בְּעַ֣ד רֵעֵ֑הוּ וַ֧יֹּסֶף יְהוָ֛ה אֶת־כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר לְאִיּ֖וֹב לְמִשְׁנֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Jehovah turned the captivity of Job, when he prayed for his friends: and Jehovah gave Job twice as much as he had before.
					\item[NIV] After Job had prayed for his friends, the LORD restored his fortunes and gave him twice as much as he had before.
					\item[NRSV] And the LORD restored the fortunes of Job when he had prayed for his friends; and the LORD gave Job twice as much as he had before.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:11} % *JOB-42-11
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-11}

			Then came there unto him all his brethren, and all his sisters, and all they that had been of his acquaintance before, and did eat bread with him in his house: and they bemoaned him, and comforted him over all the evil that the LORD had brought upon him: every man also gave him a piece of money, and every one an earring of gold.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ אֵ֠לָיו כָּל־אֶחָ֨יו אַחְיוֹתָ֜יו וְכָל־יֹדְעָ֣יו לְפָנִ֗ים וַיֹּאכְל֨וּ עִמּ֣וֹ לֶחֶם֮ בְּבֵיתוֹ֒ וַיָּנֻ֤דוּ לוֹ֙ וַיְנַחֲמ֣וּ אֹת֔וֹ עַ֚ל כָּל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הֵבִ֥יא יְהוָ֖ה עָלָ֑יו וַיִּתְּנוּ־ל֗וֹ אִ֚ישׁ קְשִׂיטָ֣ה אֶחָ֔ת וְאִ֕ישׁ נֶ֥זֶם זָהָ֖ב אֶחָֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then came there unto him all his brethren, and all his sisters, and all they that had been of his acquaintance before, and did eat bread with him in his house: and they bemoaned him, and comforted him concerning all the evil that Jehovah had brought upon him: every man also gave him a piece of money, and every one a ring of gold.
					\item[NIV] All his brothers and sisters and everyone who had known him before came and ate with him in his house. They comforted and consoled him over all the trouble the LORD had brought on him, and each one gave him a piece of silver and a gold ring.
					\item[NRSV] Then there came to him all his brothers and sisters and all who had known him before, and they ate bread with him in his house; they showed him sympathy and comforted him for all the evil that the LORD had brought upon him; and each of them gave him a piece of money and a gold ring.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:12} % *JOB-42-12
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-12}

			So the LORD blessed the latter end of Job more than his beginning: for he had fourteen thousand sheep, and six thousand camels, and a thousand yoke of oxen, and a thousand she asses.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיהוָ֗ה בֵּרַ֛ךְ אֶת־אַחֲרִ֥ית אִיּ֖וֹב מֵרֵאשִׁת֑וֹ וַֽיְהִי־ל֡וֹ אַרְבָּעָה֩ עָשָׂ֨ר אֶ֜לֶף צֹ֗אן וְשֵׁ֤שֶׁת אֲלָפִים֙ גְּמַלִּ֔ים וְאֶֽלֶף־צֶ֥מֶד בָּקָ֖ר וְאֶ֥לֶף אֲתוֹנֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Jehovah blessed the latter end of Job more than his beginning: And he had fourteen thousand sheep, and six thousand camels, and a thousand yoke of oxen, and a thousand she-asses.
					\item[NIV] The LORD blessed the latter part of Job’s life more than the former part. He had fourteen thousand sheep, six thousand camels, a thousand yoke of oxen and a thousand donkeys.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD blessed the latter days of Job more than his beginning; and he had fourteen thousand sheep, six thousand camels, a thousand yoke of oxen, and a thousand donkeys.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:13} % *JOB-42-13
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-13}

			He had also seven sons and three daughters.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיְהִי־ל֛וֹ שִׁבְעָ֥נָה בָנִ֖ים וְשָׁל֥וֹשׁ בָּנֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He had also seven sons and three daughters.
					\item[NIV] And he also had seven sons and three daughters.
					\item[NRSV] He also had seven sons and three daughters.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:14} % *JOB-42-14
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-14}

			And he called the name of the first, Jemima; and the name of the second, Kezia; and the name of the third, Kerenhappuch.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקְרָ֤א שֵׁם־הָֽאַחַת֙ יְמִימָ֔ה וְשֵׁ֥ם הַשֵּׁנִ֖ית קְצִיעָ֑ה וְשֵׁ֥ם הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֖ית קֶ֥רֶן הַפּֽוּךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he called the name of the first, Jemimah: and the name of the second, Keziah; and the name of the third, Keren-happuch.
					\item[NIV] The first daughter he named Jemimah, the second Keziah and the third Keren-Happuch.
					\item[NRSV] He named the first Jemimah, the second Keziah, and the third Keren-happuch.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:15} % *JOB-42-15
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-15}

			And in all the land were no women found so fair as the daughters of Job: and their father gave them inheritance among their brethren.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְלֹ֨א נִמְצָ֜א נָשִׁ֥ים יָפ֛וֹת כִּבְנ֥וֹת אִיּ֖וֹב בְּכָל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַיִּתֵּ֨ן לָהֶ֧ם אֲבִיהֶ֛ם נַחֲלָ֖ה בְּת֥וֹךְ אֲחֵיהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And in all the land were no women found so fair as the daughters of Job: and their father gave them inheritance among their brethren.
					\item[NIV] Nowhere in all the land were there found women as beautiful as Job’s daughters, and their father granted them an inheritance along with their brothers.
					\item[NRSV] In all the land there were no women so beautiful as Job’s daughters; and their father gave them an inheritance along with their brothers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:16} % *JOB-42-16
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-16}

			After this lived Job an hundred and forty years, and saw his sons, and his sons’ sons, even four generations.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְחִ֤י אִיּוֹב֙ אַֽחֲרֵי־זֹ֔את מֵאָ֥ה וְאַרְבָּעִ֖ים שָׁנָ֑ה וַיִּרְאֶ֗ה אֶת־בָּנָיו֙ וְאֶת־בְּנֵ֣י בָנָ֔יו אַרְבָּעָ֖ה דֹּרֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And after this Job lived a hundred and forty years, and saw his sons, and his sons' sons, even four generations.
					\item[NIV] After this, Job lived a hundred and forty years; he saw his children and their children to the fourth generation.
					\item[NRSV] After this Job lived one hundred and forty years, and saw his children, and his children’s children, four generations.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Job 42:17} % *JOB-42-17
		% % ---
			\label{JOB-42-17}

			So Job died, being old and full of days.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּ֣מָת אִיּ֔וֹב זָקֵ֖ן וּשְׂבַ֥ע יָמִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Job died, being old and full of days.
					\item[NIV] And so Job died, an old man and full of years.
					\item[NRSV] And Job died, old and full of days.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
